Test plan\n\n**Unit** (injectable clock, no database):\n- Window arithmetic: the 3rd request accepted, the 4th refused, a request after the oldest leaves the window accepted.\n- Expiry just before, at and just after T + 10 minutes (boundary per `ASM-BE-307`).\n- Counter: 4 wrong codes do not lock, the 5th does, success resets the count.\n- Lock: verification refused for 15 minutes, accepted after, correct code refused during.\n- Email normalisation (once `GAP-BE-031` is closed).\n\n**Integration** (PostgreSQL 16):\n- Send then verify end to end, creating the user exactly once across repeated sign-ins.\n- Concurrent verification of one code yields one success (INV-BE-011).\n- Concurrent sends at the limit accept at most 3 (INV-BE-013).\n- Send during a lock is accepted and verify is refused.\n- A challenge issued during a lock is usable after the lock lifts, if it is still unexpired.\n- Retention sweep deletes only rows older than their period.\n\n**Contract:**\n- Both operations match their rendered contract, including 415 on a limit breach.\n- Responses do not distinguish known from unknown addresses.\n- Statuses for the locked, wrong, expired and used cases are asserted only after `GAP-BE-038` and `GAP-BE-053` close.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.auth.gaps | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 10. Gaps\n\nItems are defined once in the project spec's open-item register and cited here.\n\n**Blocking this module**\n\n| ID | Effect |\n|---|---|\n| `Q-BE-003` | Eligibility to request, verify or receive a code. Blocks `backend.auth.authorization`. |\n| `Q-BE-005`, `CON-BE-006` (Critical) | Email transport region and PII path. Blocks the transport step of BE-AUTH-FR-001. |\n| `Q-BE-012` | Credential form. Blocks the credential step of BE-AUTH-FR-004. |\n| `CON-BE-003` (Critical) | Shared-workspace default against open eligibility. |\n\n**Cited gaps:** `GAP-BE-024` (default `ASM-BE-305`), `GAP-BE-031`, `GAP-BE-033`, `GAP-BE-035`, `GAP-BE-038`, `GAP-BE-053`, `GAP-BE-083`, `GAP-BE-085`, `GAP-BE-101`, `GAP-BE-104`, `GAP-BE-302`, `GAP-BE-304`, `GAP-BE-306`, `CON-BE-011`.\n\n**New gaps**\n\n| ID | Gap | Closing artefact |\n|---|---|---|\n| `GAP-BE-322` | The default in `ASM-BE-312` (refused sends write no row) is unconfirmed. Writing a row on refusal would keep a persistent caller blocked indefinitely. | rule from the product owner |\n| `GAP-BE-323` | Order of refusal reasons when several apply (locked, expired, consumed, wrong). | rule from the product owner |\n| `GAP-BE-324` | No idempotency or retry-safety rule for send. | decision from the product owner |\n| `GAP-BE-325` | Source of code randomness is not stated. | security requirement |\n| `GAP-BE-326` | Behaviour when the transport fails: error returned, and whether the attempt counts toward the limit. | decision from the product owner |\n| `GAP-BE-327` | Whether the existing authentication shares the users table. | description of the existing authentication (`GAP-BE-085`) |\n| `GAP-BE-328` | Whether a wrong code with no active challenge counts toward the 5. | rule from the product owner |\n\n**New assumptions**\n\n| ID | Assumption | Reason | Confirmed by |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `ASM-BE-306` | A verify attempt refused because of a lock changes neither `failed_count` nor `locked_until`. | BR-022 refuses verification during the lock and states no extension. | rule from the product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-307` | A code is expired only when the instant is strictly after issue + 10 minutes. | BR-024 and US-006-06 say \"after\". BR-020 and BR-026 say \"expires\", so `GAP-BE-306` stays open. | decision on `GAP-BE-306` |\n| `ASM-BE-308` | The wrong-code counter is a column of `login_locks`, not of `otp_challenges`. | `ASM-BE-305` keys the count per email, not per challenge. | `GAP-BE-024` closing artefact |\n| `ASM-BE-309` | Only the latest unconsumed challenge for an email is checked at verification. | No input says whether earlier codes stay valid (`GAP-BE-083`). One live code is the narrower reading. | rule from the product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-310` | Both operations return 200 on success; send returns an empty body. | No success status is stated for either. | contract decision |\n| `ASM-BE-311` | Column types: uuid keys, text email, timestamptz instants, integer counter. | PostgreSQL 16 is the platform and no types are supplied. | platform decision |\n| `ASM-BE-312` | A refused send writes no `send_attempts` row. | Otherwise a caller that keeps retrying is never released from the window. | `GAP-BE-322` |\n| `ASM-BE-313` | `failed_count` is reset to 0 when a lock is entered. | Otherwise the first wrong code after the lock would re-lock immediately. | rule from the product owner |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.auth.gaps -->"}, {"id": "backend/modules/notes-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/notes-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.frontmatter | status: DERIVED -->\n```yaml\nid: notes\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: [Q-BE-003, Q-BE-300, CON-BE-003, CON-BE-300]\nscope: [API-NOTES-232390, API-NOTES-045877, API-NOTES-847551, API-NOTES-359413, API-NOTES-150096, API-NOTES-810640, API-NOTES-917869]\n```\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.purpose | status: DERIVED -->\n## 1. Purpose\n\n**Responsibility.** The `notes` module owns the Note, Tag and NOTE_TAG data and these lifecycle operations: create, read by id, partial edit, pin and unpin (through the `pinned` field of the edit operation), tag add, tag remove, archive and unarchive. [backend.module-map; stories.md US-002-01 to US-002-06; requirements.md BR-005 to BR-008, BR-018]\n\nIt implements the following operations, referred to by id only:\n\n| Operation | Method and path | Story |\n|---|---|---|\n| `API-NOTES-232390` | POST `/notes` | US-002-01 |\n| `API-NOTES-045877` | GET `/notes/{id}` | US-002-06 |\n| `API-NOTES-847551` | PATCH `/notes/{id}` | US-002-02 |\n| `API-NOTES-359413` | POST `/notes/{id}/tags` | US-002-03 |\n| `API-NOTES-150096` | DELETE `/notes/{id}/tags/{tag}` | US-002-03 |\n| `API-NOTES-810640` | POST `/notes/{id}/archive` | US-002-04 |\n| `API-NOTES-917869` | POST `/notes/{id}/unarchive` | US-002-05 |\n\nThe tag, archive and unarchive paths are proposals because no input supplies them (`GAP-BE-303`).\n\n**Not responsible for.**\n\n- **Caller identity, code issuance, lockout and credentials.** These belong to the `auth` module. `notes` consumes caller identity only. [backend.module-map] The credential form is `Q-BE-012` and is not specified here.\n- **The note list and keyword or tag search.** These belong to the `search` module, including the archived-exclusion default and pinned-first ordering (`INV-BE-001`). `notes` only guarantees the stored `pinned` and `status` values they read. [backend.module-map; requirements.md BR-009, BR-019]\n- **Publishing the OpenAPI document, its coverage metric and the Scope section.** These belong to `api-docs`.\n- **Rejecting attachment, real-time and mobile-specific calls, and multipart payloads.** These belong to `scope-guardrail`. [stories.md US-005-02]\n- **Infrastructure region, backups, the residency gate and drift alerts.** These belong to `residency-compliance`.\n- **Any user interface.** The web-app screens in EP-006 belong to the web-app surface.\n- **Deleting notes.** No delete operation is specified. The NFR-019 retention deletion of notes is `BLOCKED` (section 9).\n- **The global error envelope and the error-code registry.** These belong to `backend.error-model`. This module refers to codes by name only.\n\n**Story coverage.** All six `US-002-*` stories are covered by requirements in section 5. `US-002-03` has acceptance criteria for add only, so tag removal is `DRAFT-UNVERIFIED` (`GAP-BE-373`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 2. Data model\n\nThe platform is PostgreSQL 16. [platform.md] No input states column types, lengths or constraints beyond those cited per row. The types below are proposals (`ASM-BE-361`). The entities and column names come from `backend.domain-model`. The migration tool is not named (`GAP-BE-089`). Migration order is in `backend.migration-plan` (migrations 001 and 002).\n\n**NOTES**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint and notes |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `id` | uuid | NOT NULL | server-generated | Primary key. Server-assigned and opaque (`ASM-BE-363`). A \"unique ID\" is returned on create. [AC-002-01-01] |\n| `title` | text | NOT NULL | none | `CHECK (btrim(title) <> '')`. Mandatory and non-empty. [BR-005] Whitespace-only counts as empty (`ASM-BE-359`). The maximum length is `GAP-BE-372`. |\n| `body` | text | NOT NULL | none | `CHECK (btrim(body) <> '')`. [BR-005] The maximum length is `GAP-BE-372`. |\n| `status` | text | NOT NULL | `'active'` | `CHECK (status IN ('active','archived'))`. A new note is active (`ASM-BE-364`). [stories.md US-002-04, US-002-05] |\n| `pinned` | boolean | NOT NULL | `false` | Default per BR-018 and AC-002-01-01. `NOT NULL` per `ASM-BE-034`. |\n| `created_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | now | Set once at create. Timezone handling is `ASM-BE-362`. |\n| `updated_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | now | `CHECK (updated_at >= created_at)`. Set on every successful mutation (`ASM-BE-362`). [AC-002-02-01] |\n\nThere is no owner column and no version column. A note has no relationship to a user in any input (`GAP-BE-003`). The ownership columns are `BLOCKED` on `CON-BE-003` and `Q-BE-003`. [requirements.md BR-031] There is no version column because concurrent PATCH uses last-write-wins (`ASM-BE-360`).\n\n**TAGS**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint and notes |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `name` | text | NOT NULL | none | Primary key. Compared exactly, with no case folding (`ASM-BE-369`). Format and length are `GAP-BE-035` and `GAP-BE-378`. |\n\nA TAGS row is created when a tag is first added to any note. A tag left with no notes is retained (`GAP-BE-378`).\n\n**NOTE_TAGS**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint and notes |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `note_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | none | FK to `notes(id)`, `ON DELETE RESTRICT` (`ASM-BE-366`). |\n| `tag_name` | text | NOT NULL | none | FK to `tags(name)`, `ON DELETE RESTRICT` (`ASM-BE-366`). |\n\nPrimary key `(note_id, tag_name)`. This composite key prevents a duplicate tag entry on a note. [stories.md US-002-03] `INV-BE-062`.\n\n**Indexes.**\n\n| Index | Query it serves |\n|---|---|\n| Primary key on `notes(id)` | Read, edit, tag, archive and unarchive by id. |\n| Primary key on `note_tags(note_id, tag_name)` | Listing a note's tags on read. Duplicate prevention on add. |\n| btree on `note_tags(tag_name, note_id)` | Reverse lookup of notes by tag. The `search` module's tag filter reads it. [stories.md US-003-02] |\n\nIndexes for keyword search, and for pinned-first and archived-excluded listing, belong to the `search` module and are not specified here. [backend.module-map]\n\n**Timestamps.** All instants are `timestamptz` and are stored as UTC instants (`ASM-BE-362`).\n\n**PII and at-rest treatment.** `title` and `body` are free text and may contain PII incidentally. No handling policy or encryption statement is supplied (`GAP-BE-017`, `GAP-BE-033`, `GAP-BE-304`). No encryption, redaction or retention claim is made here.\n\n**Region.** All note data, active and archived, is stored in the EU-region database. [requirements.md BR-001, BR-013] That provisioning is owned by `residency-compliance`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 3. State machine\n\n**States.** `active` and `archived`. [stories.md US-002-04, US-002-05; requirements.md BR-007, BR-008]\n\n**Terminal states.** There are none. Archiving is reversible and never deletes data. [BR-007, BR-008] The only possible terminal transition is the NFR-019 deletion of notes, which is `BLOCKED` (below).\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard | Effect | Result |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| none | `active` | `API-NOTES-232390` | title and body valid | row created with `pinned` default false; `created_at` and `updated_at` set | 201. [AC-002-01-01] |\n| none | none | `API-NOTES-232390` | title or body missing or empty | nothing persisted | 400. [AC-002-01-03; BR-005] |\n| `active` | `archived` | `API-NOTES-810640` | status is `active` | `status` set; all other data intact | 200. [stories.md US-002-04] |\n| `archived` | `archived` | `API-NOTES-810640` | status is `archived` | nothing changes | conflict or error; status `GAP-BE-309`; code `ALREADY_ARCHIVED` is proposed in `backend.error-model`. |\n| `archived` | `active` | `API-NOTES-917869` | status is `archived` | `status` set; title, body, tags and `pinned` as before | 200. [stories.md US-002-05; BR-008] |\n| `active` | `active` | `API-NOTES-917869` | status is `active` | not specified by any input | `GAP-BE-377`; default `ASM-BE-357`. |\n| any | same | `API-NOTES-847551` | note exists; values valid | listed fields updated; unlisted fields untouched | 200. [AC-002-02-01; BR-006] |\n| any | same | any id operation | no note with that id | nothing modified | 404. [AC-002-02-03; stories.md US-002-05, US-002-06] Derived for the tag, archive and unarchive operations. |\n| any | none | retention deletion (NFR-019) | not decided | would remove the row | `BLOCKED` on `CON-BE-300` and `Q-BE-300`. |\n\n**`pinned` is a flag, not a state.** It is orthogonal to `status`. It is changed only through `API-NOTES-232390` (initial value) and `API-NOTES-847551`. Setting `pinned` to its current value is a successful no-op (derived from \"may be set\", BR-018). Archive and unarchive neither read nor change `pinned`.\n\n**Tags are membership, not state.** Adding a tag already on the note succeeds with no change. [stories.md US-002-03] Removal of a tag not on the note is `ASM-BE-358`.\n\n**Archived notes.** No input forbids edit, pin or tag changes on an archived note. They behave as on an active note (`ASM-BE-356`, `GAP-BE-376`).\n\n**Atomicity.** The guard on status is evaluated and the transition applied as one atomic step. Two concurrent archive calls on one active note therefore yield one success and one conflict. This is derived from the conflict rule in US-002-04. Concurrent PATCH is `ASM-BE-360`.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 4. Requirements\n\nError codes named below are the proposals in `backend.error-model` and are referred to by name only. The shape of requests and responses is in the contract (section 6) and is not restated.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-001 Create a note\n\n**Given** a payload with a valid title and body,\n**When** `API-NOTES-232390` is called,\n**Then** the API returns 201 with the created note, including a unique ID, timestamps and `pinned` = false by default.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-002-01; AC-002-01-01; BR-005; BR-018.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-create-defaults` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-232390`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-002 Create a pinned note\n\n**Given** a valid payload with `pinned` = true,\n**When** `API-NOTES-232390` is called,\n**Then** the API returns 201 and the stored note has `pinned` = true.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-002-01; AC-002-01-02; BR-018.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-create-pinned` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-232390`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-003 Reject a create with no valid title\n\n**Given** a payload whose title is missing, null or empty,\n**When** `API-NOTES-232390` is called,\n**Then** the API returns 400 with a validation error (`VALIDATION_FAILED`) and no note is persisted.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-002-01; AC-002-01-03; BR-005.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-create-missing-title` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-232390`.\n- **Note.** Whitespace-only is treated as empty (`ASM-BE-359`).\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-004 Reject a create with no valid body\n\n**Given** a payload whose body is missing, null or empty,\n**When** `API-NOTES-232390` is called,\n**Then** the API returns 400 with a validation error (`VALIDATION_FAILED`) and no note is persisted.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-002-01; BR-005 (\"a note must always have a title and body at creation; empty/null payloads are rejected\"). AC-002-01-03 states the title case only, so the body case is derived from BR-005.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-create-missing-body` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-232390`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-005 Reject a non-boolean pinned on create\n\n**Given** a payload whose `pinned` is present and is not a boolean,\n**When** `API-NOTES-232390` is called,\n**Then** the API returns 400 (`VALIDATION_FAILED`) and no note is persisted.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-002-01; BR-018; NFR-012. The rule itself is `ASM-BE-368`.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-create-pinned-type` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-232390`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-006 Read a note\n\n**Given** an existing note id,\n**When** `API-NOTES-045877` is called,\n**Then** the API returns 200 with the full note, including its tags, its archive status and the `pinned` flag.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-002-06; AC-002-06-01 as given by the story's first criterion; BR-018.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-get-full` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-045877`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-007 Read a note that does not exist\n\n**Given** an id for which no note exists,\n**When** `API-NOTES-045877` is called,\n**Then** the API returns 404 (`NOT_FOUND`) and exposes no partial data.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-002-06 second criterion.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-get-not-found` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-045877`.\n- **Note.** The story also says \"deleted\". No delete operation is in scope (`ASM-BE-365`), so a deleted note is treated as never-existing.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-008 Edit a note\n\n**Given** an existing note id and valid updated fields,\n**When** `API-NOTES-847551` is called,\n**Then** the API returns 200 with the updated note and an updated timestamp.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-002-02; AC-002-02-01.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-patch-update` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-847551`.\n- **Note.** The story names PUT/PATCH. Only PATCH is specified (`GAP-BE-309`).\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-009 Partial update leaves other fields untouched\n\n**Given** an existing note and a PATCH containing only the `pinned` field,\n**When** `API-NOTES-847551` is called,\n**Then** the API returns 200, `pinned` is updated, and title, body and tags are unchanged.\nMore generally, no field absent from a PATCH is overwritten.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-002-02; AC-002-02-02; BR-006; BR-018; `INV-BE-008`.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-patch-pinned-only`, `test-notes-patch-partial-preserves` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-847551`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-010 Edit a note that does not exist\n\n**Given** a note id for which no note exists,\n**When** `API-NOTES-847551` is called,\n**Then** the API returns 404 (`NOT_FOUND`) and no data is modified.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-002-02; AC-002-02-03.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-patch-not-found` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-847551`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-011 Reject an invalid edit\n\n**Given** an existing note and a PATCH that sets `title` or `body` to null or empty, or `pinned` to a non-boolean,\n**When** `API-NOTES-847551` is called,\n**Then** the API returns 400 (`VALIDATION_FAILED`) and no data is modified.\n\n- **Traces to.** BR-005 applied to every stored value (`ASM-BE-359`); BR-006; US-002-02. `INV-BE-060`, `INV-BE-064`.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-patch-invalid-values` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-847551`.\n- **Note.** Empty PATCH bodies, unknown fields and attempts to set immutable fields are `GAP-BE-374`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-012 Concurrent edits\n\n**Given** two clients submit PATCH requests to the same note concurrently,\n**When** both requests are processed,\n**Then** the API applies a standard last-write response, with no live broadcast and no merge logic.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-005-03 second criterion (owned by `scope-guardrail`; the behaviour lives in this module's PATCH handler).\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-patch-concurrent` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-847551`.\n- **Note.** The story allows \"last-write/conflict\". Last-write is chosen by default (`ASM-BE-360`, `Q-BE-354`, `GAP-BE-379`).\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-013 Add a tag\n\n**Given** an existing note and a tag name,\n**When** `API-NOTES-359413` is called,\n**Then** the API returns 200 and the note's tag list includes the new tag.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-002-03; AC for add as given by the story's first criterion; NFR-012.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-tag-add` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-359413`.\n- **Note.** Tag name format and length are `GAP-BE-035`. The create-with-tags case is `ASM-BE-367`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-014 Add a tag already on the note\n\n**Given** a tag that already exists on the note,\n**When** `API-NOTES-359413` is called with the same tag,\n**Then** the API returns 200 and creates no duplicate tag entry.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-002-03 second criterion. `INV-BE-062`.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-tag-add-idempotent` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-359413`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-015 Add a tag to a note that does not exist\n\n**Given** an id for which no note exists,\n**When** `API-NOTES-359413` is called,\n**Then** the API returns 404 (`NOT_FOUND`) and creates no tag or link.\n\n- **Traces to.** Derived from the 404 rule in US-002-02 and US-002-05. No input states it for tags.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-tag-add-note-not-found` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-359413`.\n- **Status.** Derived, not cited.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-016 Remove a tag\n\n**Given** an existing note carrying a tag,\n**When** `API-NOTES-150096` is called for that tag,\n**Then** the tag is no longer in the note's tag list, and the note's other data is unchanged.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-002-03 (the story title says \"add or remove\"; it has no removal criteria); US-006-04 second criterion (web-app expectation).\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-tag-remove` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-150096`.\n- **Status.** The success status is not stated (`GAP-BE-373`). A tag not on the note returns 404 (`ASM-BE-358`).\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-017 Archive a note\n\n**Given** an active, existing note,\n**When** `API-NOTES-810640` is called,\n**Then** the API returns 200, marks the note archived, and its data remains intact.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-002-04; BR-007.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-archive` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-810640`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-018 Archive an already-archived note\n\n**Given** an already-archived note,\n**When** `API-NOTES-810640` is called again,\n**Then** the API returns a conflict or error response indicating the note is already archived (`ALREADY_ARCHIVED`), and nothing changes.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-002-04 second criterion.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-archive-twice` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-810640`.\n- **Status.** The status code is not stated (`GAP-BE-309`).\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-019 Archive a note that does not exist\n\n**Given** an id for which no note exists,\n**When** `API-NOTES-810640` is called,\n**Then** the API returns 404 (`NOT_FOUND`).\n\n- **Traces to.** Derived from the 404 rule in US-002-05. No input states it for archive.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-archive-not-found` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-810640`.\n- **Status.** Derived, not cited.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-020 Unarchive a note\n\n**Given** an archived note,\n**When** `API-NOTES-917869` is called,\n**Then** the API returns 200, the note's status changes to active, and all original data is preserved, including title, body, tags and `pinned`.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-002-05; BR-008; `INV-BE-061`.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-unarchive-restores`, `test-notes-archive-roundtrip` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-917869`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-021 Unarchive a note that does not exist\n\n**Given** an id for which no note exists,\n**When** `API-NOTES-917869` is called,\n**Then** the API returns 404 (`NOT_FOUND`).\n\n- **Traces to.** US-002-05 second criterion.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-unarchive-not-found` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-NOTES-917869`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-022 Validate and sanitise input\n\n**Given** any request to a note, tag, archive or unarchive operation,\n**When** the request is processed,\n**Then** the title, body and tag fields are validated and sanitised, so that injection payloads are neither executed nor stored as executable content.\n\n- **Traces to.** NFR-012 (target: 0 critical injection vulnerabilities per release).\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-injection-payloads` (planned), plus the release security scan named in NFR-012.\n- **API.** all seven operations.\n- **Note.** No library or technique is named in any input (`GAP-BE-089`).\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-023 Require authentication\n\n**Given** a request to any of the seven operations with no valid credential,\n**When** the request reaches the operation,\n**Then** the API returns 401 (`UNAUTHENTICATED`) and nothing is read or changed.\n\n- **Traces to.** Derived from US-006-06, US-004-03 and `backend.auth-model`, where unauthenticated callers are refused on note operations by derivation.\n- **Verification.** `test-notes-requires-auth` (planned).\n- **API.** all seven operations.\n- **Status.** Derived, not cited. The credential form is `Q-BE-012`.\n\n### BE-NOTES-FR-024 Note access rule\n\n`BLOCKED`. The rule for which authenticated caller may read or change which note (403 and the ownership predicate) cannot be written. BR-031 is corrupted as supplied. BR-030 leaves open who may obtain a credential at all. The shared-workspace default of `Q-BE-001` is a placeholder only, and `CON-BE-003` (Critical) calls it into question.\n\n- **Traces to.** BR-030; BR-031.\n- **Unblocked by.** The answer to `Q-BE-003` and a corrected BR-031.\n- **API.** all seven operations.\n\n### Story to requirement mapping\n\n| Story | Requirements |\n|---|---|\n| US-002-01 | FR-001, FR-002, FR-003, FR-004, FR-005 |\n| US-002-02 | FR-008, FR-009, FR-010, FR-011, FR-012 |\n| US-002-03 | FR-013, FR-014, FR-015, FR-016 |\n| US-002-04 | FR-017, FR-018, FR-019 |\n| US-002-05 | FR-020, FR-021 |\n| US-002-06 | FR-006, FR-007 |\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-NOTES-232390` | POST | `/notes` | `notes` | 201 | 400, 401, 403, 415 | Create a note with a mandatory non-empty title and body, optionally pinned. |\n| `API-NOTES-045877` | GET | `/notes/{id}` | `notes` | 200 | 401, 403, 404 | Read a single note by id. |\n| `API-NOTES-847551` | PATCH | `/notes/{id}` | `notes` | 200 | 400, 401, 403, 404, 415 | Partially edit a note's title or body, and pin or unpin it via the pinned field; omitted fields are never overwritten. |\n| `API-NOTES-810640` | POST | `/notes/{id}/archive` | `notes` | 200 | 401, 403, 404, 409 | Archive a note, hiding it from default listings without deleting any data. Path proposed (GAP-BE-303). |\n| `API-NOTES-359413` | POST | `/notes/{id}/tags` | `notes` | 200 | 400, 401, 403, 404, 415 | Add a tag to a note; adding an existing tag creates no duplicate. Path proposed because none is supplied (GAP-BE-303). |\n| `API-NOTES-150096` | DELETE | `/notes/{id}/tags/{tag}` | `notes` | 200 | 401, 403, 404 | Remove a tag from a note. Path proposed because none is supplied (GAP-BE-303). |\n| `API-NOTES-917869` | POST | `/notes/{id}/unarchive` | `notes` | 200 | 401, 403, 404 | Unarchive a note, restoring it to active with all data preserved. Path proposed (GAP-BE-303). |\n\n### `API-NOTES-232390` \u2014 POST `/notes`\n\nCreate a note with a mandatory non-empty title and body, optionally pinned.\n\n**Request:** \n- `title` (string, required): Note title; mandatory and non-empty (BR-005, AC-002-01-03).\n- `body` (string, required): Note body; mandatory and non-empty (BR-005).\n- `pinned` (boolean): Whether the note is pinned; defaults to false (BR-018).\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Identifier of the created note.\n- `title` (string, required): Note title.\n- `body` (string, required): Note body.\n- `status` (string, required): Note status, active or archived; a new note is active.\n- `pinned` (boolean, required): Pinned flag.\n- `tags` (array, required): Tag names applied to the note.\n- `created_at` (string, required): Creation timestamp.\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp.\n\n### `API-NOTES-045877` \u2014 GET `/notes/{id}`\n\nRead a single note by id.\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier.\n- `title` (string, required): Note title.\n- `body` (string, required): Note body.\n- `status` (string, required): Note status, active or archived.\n- `pinned` (boolean, required): Pinned flag.\n- `tags` (array, required): Tag names applied to the note.\n- `created_at` (string, required): Creation timestamp.\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp.\n\n### `API-NOTES-847551` \u2014 PATCH `/notes/{id}`\n\nPartially edit a note's title or body, and pin or unpin it via the pinned field; omitted fields are never overwritten.\n\n**Request:** \n- `title` (string): New title; omitted means unchanged (BR-006).\n- `body` (string): New body; omitted means unchanged (BR-006).\n- `pinned` (boolean): true pins, false unpins the note (BR-018).\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier.\n- `title` (string, required): Note title after the update.\n- `body` (string, required): Note body after the update.\n- `status` (string, required): Note status, active or archived.\n- `pinned` (boolean, required): Pinned flag after the update.\n- `tags` (array, required): Tag names applied to the note.\n- `created_at` (string, required): Creation timestamp.\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp.\n\n### `API-NOTES-810640` \u2014 POST `/notes/{id}/archive`\n\nArchive a note, hiding it from default listings without deleting any data. Path proposed (GAP-BE-303).\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier.\n- `status` (string, required): Note status, now archived.\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp.\n\n### `API-NOTES-359413` \u2014 POST `/notes/{id}/tags`\n\nAdd a tag to a note; adding an existing tag creates no duplicate. Path proposed because none is supplied (GAP-BE-303).\n\n**Request:** \n- `name` (string, required): Tag name to add; format and length rules not supplied.\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier.\n- `tags` (array, required): Tag names on the note after the addition, without duplicates.\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp.\n\n### `API-NOTES-150096` \u2014 DELETE `/notes/{id}/tags/{tag}`\n\nRemove a tag from a note. Path proposed because none is supplied (GAP-BE-303).\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier.\n- `tags` (array, required): Tag names remaining on the note.\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp.\n\n### `API-NOTES-917869` \u2014 POST `/notes/{id}/unarchive`\n\nUnarchive a note, restoring it to active with all data preserved. Path proposed (GAP-BE-303).\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `id` (string, required): Note identifier.\n- `status` (string, required): Note status, now active.\n- `updated_at` (string, required): Last update timestamp.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.invariants | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 6. Invariants\n\nThese hold after every operation of this module. The project-level `INV-BE-008` (PATCH never overwrites an absent field) and `INV-BE-009` (archive and unarchive preserve all note data) also apply and are not restated.\n\n- **`INV-BE-060`.** Every stored note has a non-empty title and a non-empty body. [requirements.md BR-005]\n- **`INV-BE-061`.** `status` is exactly `active` or `archived`. Archive and unarchive change `status` and `updated_at` only; title, body, tags, `pinned` and `created_at` are unchanged. [requirements.md BR-007, BR-008]\n- **`INV-BE-062`.** No `(note_id, tag_name)` pair appears twice. [stories.md US-002-03]\n- **`INV-BE-063`.** `pinned` is never null; it is a boolean. [requirements.md BR-018; `ASM-BE-034`]\n- **`INV-BE-064`.** An operation that returns a 4xx response has modified no stored note, tag or link. [AC-002-01-03; AC-002-02-03] This is the \"no note persisted\" and \"no data modified\" rule generalised.\n- **`INV-BE-065`.** `updated_at` is at or after `created_at`, and is advanced by every successful change to the note (`ASM-BE-362`).\n- **`INV-BE-066`.** Note ids are unique. No operation of this module changes an id. [AC-002-01-01; `ASM-BE-363`]\n\n`INV-BE-001` (pinned notes sort first in list and search) depends on `INV-BE-063` and is enforced by the `search` module.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.authorization | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 7. Authorization\n\n**Source.** This section applies `backend.auth-model`. No input defines a roles model (`GAP-BE-085`). A role has no permission on an operation unless an input grants it (`ASM-BE-302`).\n\n| Operation | Unauthenticated caller | Team member | Other roles |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-NOTES-232390` create | D* (401) | `B` | - |\n| `API-NOTES-045877` read | D* (401) | `B` | - |\n| `API-NOTES-847551` edit and pin | D* (401) | `B` | - |\n| `API-NOTES-359413` tag add | D* (401) | `B` | - |\n| `API-NOTES-150096` tag remove | D* (401) | `B` | - |\n| `API-NOTES-810640` archive | D* (401) | `B` | - |\n| `API-NOTES-917869` unarchive | D* (401) | `B` | - |\n\nLegend: `D*` refused, derived from US-006-06 and US-004-03 (`BE-NOTES-FR-023`). `B` blocked on `CON-BE-003` and `Q-BE-003`. `-` no grant stated, so refused under `ASM-BE-302`.\n\nStories grant these actions to a \"team member\". Who counts as a team member is open, so the cell stays `B`, not `A`.\n\n**Ownership predicate.** `BLOCKED` on `CON-BE-003`, `Q-BE-003` and the corrupted BR-031. The placeholder default is `Q-BE-001`: notes form a shared workspace and any authenticated team member may read and write any note. That default is not applied as a rule here, and no ownership column exists in section 2 until it is decided.\n\n**What would unblock it.** The answer to `Q-BE-003`, and a corrected BR-031 that states the access rule.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.jobs | status: BLOCKED -->\n## 8. Jobs\n\n**Blocked.** The only scheduled work any input implies for this module is the retention deletion in NFR-019. As supplied it reads positionally \"notes 1 days\" (`ASM-BE-303`), which deletes every note after one day. That contradicts BR-007, BR-008 and NFR-005 (`CON-BE-300`, Critical). Whether it is intended is `Q-BE-300` (blocking). The mechanism, schedule and effect on references are `GAP-BE-101`.\n\n**Would unblock it.** An answer to `Q-BE-300`, a corrected NFR-019, and an operations decision on the scheduler.\n\n**Not blocked, stated for completeness.**\n\n- No sweep, retry or other background work is specified for archive, unarchive, tags or pinning.\n- **Idempotency.** Tag add is idempotent by rule (FR-014). Archive is deliberately not idempotent (FR-018). PATCH sets values and repeating it gives the same stored result. No idempotency key is supplied for create, so a retried POST can create a second note (`GAP-BE-375`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 9. Test plan\n\nAll tests are planned. None exist.\n\n**Unit.**\n\n| Test | Covers |\n|---|---|\n| `test-notes-validate-title-body` | FR-003, FR-004, FR-011; `INV-BE-060` (empty, null, whitespace-only) |\n| `test-notes-validate-pinned-type` | FR-005, FR-011; `INV-BE-063` |\n| `test-notes-partial-merge` | FR-009; `INV-BE-008` |\n| `test-notes-state-guard` | FR-017, FR-018, FR-020; state machine guards |\n| `test-notes-updated-at-advances` | `INV-BE-065` |\n\n**Integration** (PostgreSQL 16 test database).\n\n| Test | Covers |\n|---|---|\n| `test-notes-create-defaults`, `test-notes-create-pinned`, `test-notes-create-missing-title`, `test-notes-create-missing-body`, `test-notes-create-pinned-type` | FR-001 to FR-005 |\n| `test-notes-get-full`, `test-notes-get-not-found` | FR-006, FR-007 |\n| `test-notes-patch-update`, `test-notes-patch-pinned-only`, `test-notes-patch-partial-preserves`, `test-notes-patch-not-found`, `test-notes-patch-invalid-values`, `test-notes-patch-concurrent` | FR-008 to FR-012 |\n| `test-notes-tag-add`, `test-notes-tag-add-idempotent`, `test-notes-tag-add-note-not-found`, `test-notes-tag-remove` | FR-013 to FR-016; `INV-BE-062` |\n| `test-notes-archive`, `test-notes-archive-twice`, `test-notes-archive-not-found`, `test-notes-unarchive-restores`, `test-notes-unarchive-not-found`, `test-notes-archive-roundtrip` | FR-017 to FR-021; `INV-BE-061` |\n| `test-notes-no-write-on-4xx` | `INV-BE-064`: after each 4xx case, row counts and stored values are unchanged |\n| `test-notes-requires-auth` | FR-023 |\n| `test-notes-injection-payloads` | FR-022; NFR-012 |\n| `test-notes-concurrent-archive` | state-machine atomicity: one success, one conflict |\n\n**Contract.**\n\n| Test | Covers |\n|---|---|\n| `test-notes-contract-conformance` | Each of the seven operations answers as the rendered OpenAPI entry says, including every documented error status. [requirements.md BR-012, NFR-008] |\n| `test-notes-crud-pass-rate` | NFR-005: 100% functional-test pass rate for create, edit, get, archive, unarchive, with zero data loss |\n| `test-notes-search-reads-pinned-status` | The `search` module's reads of `pinned` and `status` match what this module stores. Shared with the `search` module's plan. |\n\n**Not testable yet.** The note access rule (FR-024, `Q-BE-003`) and the retention deletion (section 8, `Q-BE-300`). Tests for them are written after those items are decided.\n\n**Release gates.** The injection scan named in NFR-012. No latency, throughput or availability target is supplied for these operations beyond the 99.9% monthly figure for CRUD and search in NFR-013, so no performance test is specified (`GAP-BE-380`).\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.notes.gaps | status: DERIVED -->\n## 10. Gaps, contradictions, assumptions and questions\n\n### Contradiction check\n\n| Direction | Result |\n|---|---|\n| story and design | The design brief contains AC-002-01-01 to AC-002-01-03 only for this module. They match US-002-01. No contradiction found. |\n| story and requirement | No new contradiction. US-002-06 says \"deleted\", while BR-007 excludes hard delete (handled by `ASM-BE-365`). |\n| requirement and requirement | `CON-BE-300` (BR-007, BR-008, NFR-005 against NFR-019 as supplied). Carried from the project spec. |\n| design and design | None found. |\n\nNo new `CON-` was minted. Open contradictions that affect this module: `CON-BE-003` (Critical), `CON-BE-300` (Critical), `CON-BE-019` (the change request carries three labels; no identifier renumbered).\n\n### Gaps\n\n| ID | What is missing | Degrades | What was done | Closing artefact |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `GAP-BE-372` | Maximum length of title and body, and any other field constraint. US-004-02 says such constraints are documented but gives no value. | data model; FR-003, FR-004, FR-011 | No length limit is asserted. | Field-level requirements. |\n| `GAP-BE-373` | Success status for tag removal, and behaviour on removing a tag not on the note. US-002-03 has criteria for add only. | FR-016 | FR-016 is `DRAFT-UNVERIFIED`; absent tag is 404 under `ASM-BE-358`. | Acceptance criteria for removal. |\n| `GAP-BE-374` | Behaviour of PATCH with an empty body, with unknown fields, and with attempts to set `id`, `status`, `created_at` or `updated_at`. Whether PATCH accepts a `tags` field (US-002-02 says \"or tags\"). | FR-008, FR-011 | Tags change only through the two tag operations; the rest is not asserted. | Contract decision for PATCH. |\n| `GAP-BE-375` | No idempotency key or duplicate-create protection for POST `/notes`. | FR-001; section 8 | A retried create can create a second note. | A decision on client-supplied idempotency keys. |\n| `GAP-BE-376` | Whether edit, pin and tag changes are allowed on an archived note. | state machine; FR-008, FR-013 | Allowed under `ASM-BE-356`. | Answer to `Q-BE-351`. |\n| `GAP-BE-377` | Behaviour of unarchive on an active note. | state machine; FR-020 | Same conflict as archive of an archived note under `ASM-BE-357`; no requirement written. | Answer to `Q-BE-352`. |\n| `GAP-BE-378` | Tag name normalisation (case, whitespace) and whether a tag with no notes is kept. | data model; FR-013 | Exact comparison (`ASM-BE-369`); orphan tags retained. | Tag naming rule from the product owner. |\n| `GAP-BE-379` | Whether concurrent PATCH returns last-write or a conflict. US-005-03 allows either. | FR-012 | Last-write (`ASM-BE-360`). | Answer to `Q-BE-354`. |\n| `GAP-BE-380` | No rate-limit class and no latency or throughput target for note operations beyond NFR-013's 99.9% monthly. | contract; test plan | None asserted. | NFR figures for note operations. |\n\nCarried from the project spec and affecting this module: `GAP-BE-003`, `GAP-BE-017`, `GAP-BE-033`, `GAP-BE-035`, `GAP-BE-085`, `GAP-BE-089`, `GAP-BE-101`, `GAP-BE-300`, `GAP-BE-303`, `GAP-BE-304`, `GAP-BE-309`, `GAP-BE-310` (within-group ordering, owned by `search`).\n\n### Assumptions\n\n| ID | Assumption | Reason | Confirmed by |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `ASM-BE-356` | Edit, pin and tag changes on an archived note behave as on an active note. | No input forbids them, and BR-008 says only that unarchive restores the prior state. | `Q-BE-351` |\n| `ASM-BE-357` | Unarchive of an active note returns the same conflict as archive of an archived note. | Mirrors US-002-04's second criterion. Nothing else is cited. | `Q-BE-352` |\n| `ASM-BE-358` | Removing a tag not on the note returns 404 (`NOT_FOUND`). | Add is explicitly idempotent while removal has no criteria. A 404 keeps the two distinguishable. | `Q-BE-353` |\n| `ASM-BE-359` | A title or body of whitespace only counts as empty. | BR-005 says \"empty/null\" rejected; a whitespace-only value carries no content. | Product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-360` | Concurrent PATCH is last-write-wins; there is no version column. | US-005-03 allows last-write or conflict and neither is specified further. | `Q-BE-354` |\n| `ASM-BE-361` | Column types: `uuid` for `id`, `text` for title, body and tag name, `boolean`, `timestamptz`. | Platform is PostgreSQL 16; no input states types. | Platform decision |\n| `ASM-BE-362` | Timestamps are stored as UTC instants. `updated_at` is set on PATCH, archive, unarchive, and on any tag change that alters the tag set. A repeated add of an existing tag changes nothing. | AC-002-02-01 requires an updated timestamp on edit only. | Confirmation |\n| `ASM-BE-363` | The note id is server-assigned and opaque; clients cannot supply it. | AC-002-01-01 says the API returns a unique ID. | Confirmation |\n| `ASM-BE-364` | A new note has status `active`. | US-002-04 archives an \"active, existing note\" and the create criteria carry no status. | Confirmation |\n| `ASM-BE-365` | No delete operation for notes is in scope. \"Deleted\" in US-002-06 is treated as never-existing. | BR-007 keeps data; no input describes a delete. | `Q-BE-300` |\n| `ASM-BE-366` | `note_tags` foreign keys use `ON DELETE RESTRICT`. | No delete is in scope (`ASM-BE-365`), so the choice has no visible effect. | When deletion is specified |\n| `ASM-BE-367` | POST `/notes` accepts no tags and creates no tag rows. | The create criteria carry title, body and `pinned` only. | Contract decision |\n| `ASM-BE-368` | A non-boolean `pinned` on create or PATCH is refused with 400. | Validation applies to every typed field, as in AC-002-01-03 and NFR-012. | Confirmation |\n| `ASM-BE-369` | Tag names are compared exactly, with no case folding. | No normalisation rule is supplied. Example tag names in the stories are lowercase. | `GAP-BE-378` |\n\nProject-level assumptions used: `ASM-BE-034`, `ASM-BE-302`, `ASM-BE-303`.\n\n### Questions\n\nUnattended run: degradable questions take their stated default silently; blocking questions halt. None is asked in this document.\n\n| ID | Class | Question | Default |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `Q-BE-351` | degradable | Are edit, pin and tag changes allowed on an archived note? | Allowed (`ASM-BE-356`). |\n| `Q-BE-352` | degradable | What does unarchive of an active note return? | Conflict, as in `ASM-BE-357`. |\n| `Q-BE-353` | degradable | What does removing a tag that is not on the note return? | 404 (`ASM-BE-358`). |\n| `Q-BE-354` | degradable | Does concurrent PATCH return last-write or a conflict? | Last-write (`ASM-BE-360`). |\n\nOpen blocking items that apply to this module and are owned by the project spec: `Q-BE-003`, `Q-BE-300`, `CON-BE-003`, `CON-BE-300`. Under core rule 10.1 they halt the run. Everything independent of them is written above. The access rule (FR-024), the ownership predicate and the retention job are `BLOCKED` in place.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.notes.gaps -->"}, {"id": "backend/modules/search-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/search-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.search.frontmatter | status: DERIVED -->\n## Frontmatter\n\n```yaml\nid: search\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: [CON-BE-003, Q-BE-003]\nscope: [API-SEARCH-909642, API-SEARCH-580505]\n```\n\n`blocked_by` affects `backend.search.authorization` and `BE-SEARCH-FR-022` only. Every other part of the module is written, with its open items marked in place. [backend.auth-model; backend.overview open-item register]\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.purpose | status: DERIVED -->\n## 1. Purpose\n\n**Responsibility.** `search` is the read-only query module over notes. [backend.module-map] It owns:\n\n1. **Keyword search** over note title and content, as `API-SEARCH-580505`. [stories.md US-003-01]\n2. **Tag filtering** by one or more tags, and keyword plus tag filters in one request. [stories.md US-003-02, US-003-04; requirements.md BR-010]\n3. **Pagination** with a total count. Unbounded result sets are not permitted. [stories.md US-003-05; requirements.md BR-011]\n4. **Archived exclusion** by default, with opt-in inclusion through `include_archived`. [stories.md US-003-06; requirements.md BR-009]\n5. **Pinned-first ordering** in search and list results. [requirements.md BR-019]\n6. **The note list**, `API-SEARCH-909642`. It is paginated, puts pinned notes first and excludes archived notes by default. [operation record for `API-SEARCH-909642`; requirements.md BR-019; ASM-BE-409, ASM-BE-412]\n7. **Search performance and degradation.** p95 latency under 300 ms at 50 requests per second. Return 503 with a retry-after header when the search index is degraded or unavailable. [stories.md US-003-03; requirements.md NFR-006, NFR-007]\n\n**Not responsible for.**\n\n- Any write to a note: create, edit, pin or unpin by PATCH, tag add or remove, archive, unarchive. These belong to `notes`.\n- The definition of Note, Tag and NOTE_TAG storage. `notes` owns them. This module reads them. [backend.module-map]\n- Sign-in, credential issuance and the existing authentication method. `auth` owns them. Search depends on `auth` for caller identity only. [backend.module-map; `GAP-BE-085`]\n- Deciding who may see which note. That rule is open and is not this module's to invent (`CON-BE-003`, `Q-BE-003`).\n- Retention and deletion of notes (NFR-019, `CON-BE-300`). If notes can be deleted, search simply must not return them.\n- Publishing the OpenAPI document, its examples and the coverage metric. `api-docs` owns them.\n- Rejecting attachment, real-time and mobile-specific calls. `scope-guardrail` owns that.\n- The search box, the \"no results\" message and any screen. The web-app surface owns them. [stories.md US-006-03]\n- Logging and classifying stakeholder requests. [requirements.md BR-017]\n\n**Stories owned.** US-003-01 to US-003-06. [RUN-CONTEXT assignment; stories.md EP-003]\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 2. Data model\n\n**Owned tables: none.** Search reads the note tables and writes nothing. [backend.module-map: \"Read-only query over Note\"; `ASM-BE-406`]\n\n**Data read.** Names come from the project domain model. Types, nullability, defaults and constraints are defined by `notes` and are not restated here. [backend.domain-model]\n\n| Source | Columns read | Used for |\n|---|---|---|\n| NOTE | `id`, `title`, `body`, `status`, `pinned` | keyword match, archived filter, pinned-first ordering, tie-break, result content |\n| TAG / NOTE_TAG | `tag_name`, `note_id` | tag filter and join |\n\n\"Content\" in US-003-01 is read as the note `body` (`ASM-BE-407`).\n\n**Index requirements.** This module needs the following indexes. They are placed on the `notes` migrations 001 and 002. [backend.migration-plan]\n\n| # | Index | Query served | Status |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | A text-match index over `title` and `body` | keyword match for `q` at about 100k notes with the p95 target [stories.md US-003-03 AC2; requirements.md NFR-006] | form undecided: `GAP-BE-422`; matching rule `GAP-BE-423` |\n| 2 | NOTE on (`status`, `pinned`, `id`) | archived exclusion, pinned-first ordering and the tie-break [requirements.md BR-009, BR-019; `ASM-BE-408`] | proposal `ASM-BE-417`; the final key set depends on `GAP-BE-310` and `GAP-BE-426` |\n| 3 | NOTE_TAG on (`tag_name`, `note_id`) | tag filter and its join to NOTE [stories.md US-003-02] | proposal `ASM-BE-417` |\n\nNo input names an index kind, so none is named here.\n\n**Search index freshness.** US-003-03 refers to a \"search index\" that can be degraded or unavailable, and to \"indexed lookup\". [stories.md US-003-03 AC2, AC3] Whether it is a structure separate from the note tables, how it stays in step with note writes, and whether a note is searchable immediately after it is written are not stated. See `GAP-BE-422`.\n\n**Referential integrity, timestamps.** None owned.\n\n**PII.** Note `title` and `body` are free text and may contain PII incidentally. [backend.domain-model] The query text `q` is caller-typed text and may contain PII. Whether it may be logged is not stated (`GAP-BE-433`). No claim of encryption or redaction is made. [backend.data-lifecycle; `GAP-BE-304`]\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 3. State machine\n\n### 3.1 Entity status\n\nThis module owns no entity with a status column. It filters on the note `status` that `notes` owns. [backend.domain-model]\n\n| Note status | Returned by default | Returned with `include_archived=true` |\n|---|---|---|\n| `active` | yes | yes |\n| `archived` | no | yes |\n\n[requirements.md BR-009, \"Search query default filter: status=active\"; stories.md US-003-06]\n\n### 3.2 Search index availability\n\nStates:\n\n- `SERVING`: the index answers queries.\n- `DEGRADED`: the index is degraded or unavailable. US-003-03 gives one response for both, so they are one state.\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard | Effect |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `SERVING` | `DEGRADED` | fault detected | none | each search request returns 503 with a retry-after header [stories.md US-003-03 AC3] |\n| `DEGRADED` | `SERVING` | recovery detected | none | search requests are answered normally |\n\nNeither state is terminal. How a fault is detected is not stated (`GAP-BE-422`). The list operation does not read the search index and is not affected (`ASM-BE-416`).\n\n### 3.3 Request evaluation\n\nA request holds no state between calls. Every request ends in exactly one outcome. The order of checks is `ASM-BE-415`, because no input states it.\n\n| Step | Check | Outcome on failure |\n|---|---|---|\n| 1 | caller is authenticated | 401 `UNAUTHENTICATED` (`BE-SEARCH-FR-021`) |\n| 2 | caller may read notes | 403 `FORBIDDEN`, rule `BLOCKED` on `Q-BE-003` |\n| 3 | parameters are valid | 400 `VALIDATION_FAILED` |\n| 4 | index is `SERVING` (search only) | 503 `SEARCH_UNAVAILABLE` |\n| 5 | filter, order, slice, count | 200 |\n\nCodes and statuses are those proposed in `backend.error-model`. There are no illegal transitions.\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 4. Requirements\n\nParameter names `q`, `tags`, `page`, `limit` and `include_archived` come from the acceptance criteria. Everything else about the request and response shape is in the contract rendered by DevAgent and is not restated.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-001 Keyword search\n\n**Given** notes whose title or body matches `q` under the matching rule, and an authenticated caller,\n**When** `GET /notes/search` is called with a non-empty `q`,\n**Then** the response is 200 with the matching notes, pinned notes first, then ordered by relevance.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-003-01 AC1; BR-019.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-keyword-match` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n- **Status.** `DRAFT-UNVERIFIED`: `GAP-BE-423`, `GAP-BE-426`, `ASM-BE-407`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-002 Empty result is success\n\n**Given** no note matches the supplied criteria,\n**When** the search is executed,\n**Then** the response is 200 with an empty array, not an error.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-003-01 AC2; US-003-02 AC2; US-003-04 AC2.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-empty-result` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-003 A request must carry a search criterion\n\n**Given** a search request,\n**When** it carries neither `q` nor `tags`, or carries `q` present but empty,\n**Then** the response is 400 `VALIDATION_FAILED` and no search runs.\n**And when** `q` is absent and `tags` is present, the request is a tag-only search (`BE-SEARCH-FR-004`).\n\n- **Traces to.** US-003-01 AC3; US-003-02 AC1; `CON-BE-401`.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-missing-q-400`, `test-search-tags-only-ok` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n- **Status.** `DRAFT-UNVERIFIED`. This is the reading chosen under `CON-BE-401`, with default `Q-BE-403`. A `q` made only of whitespace is `GAP-BE-429`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-004 Tag filter\n\n**Given** notes tagged `project-x`,\n**When** `GET /notes/search?tags=project-x` is called,\n**Then** only notes carrying that tag are returned, with status 200. A tag with no matching notes gives `BE-SEARCH-FR-002`.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-003-02 AC1, AC2.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-tag-filter` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-005 More than one tag\n\n**Given** a `tags` value naming two or more tags,\n**When** the search is executed,\n**Then** only notes carrying all named tags are returned.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-003-02 (\"one or more tags\").\n- **Verification.** `test-search-multi-tag` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n- **Status.** `DRAFT-UNVERIFIED`: AND combination is `ASM-BE-413` (`Q-BE-402`). The way several tags are written in `tags` is `GAP-BE-425`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-006 Invalid tag characters\n\n**Given** a `tags` value containing invalid characters,\n**When** the search request is made,\n**Then** the response is 400 with an error detail.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-003-02 AC3.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-tags-invalid-400` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n- **Status.** `BLOCKED`. \"Invalid characters\" cannot be defined until the tag name format is stated (`GAP-BE-035`, `GAP-BE-425`).\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-007 Combined keyword and tag filters\n\n**Given** `q` and `tags` both supplied,\n**When** the search is executed,\n**Then** only notes matching both criteria are returned. If the keyword matches but the tag filter excludes every such note, the result is 200 with an empty array.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-003-04 AC1, AC2; BR-010.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-combined` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-008 Malformed filter combination\n\n**Given** a malformed filter combination,\n**When** the search request is made,\n**Then** the response is 400 with error details.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-003-04 AC3.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-malformed-filter-400` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n- **Status.** `BLOCKED`. No input says what makes a combination malformed (`GAP-BE-431`).\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-009 Paginated subset and total count\n\n**Given** more matching notes than the page size,\n**When** `page` and `limit` are supplied,\n**Then** the correct subset for that page is returned together with a total count of all matching notes.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-003-05 AC1; BR-011.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-pagination-subset` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`, `API-SEARCH-909642`.\n- **Status.** `DRAFT-UNVERIFIED`: page numbering is `ASM-BE-411`. The list operation's parameter names are `ASM-BE-409`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-010 Page beyond the results\n\n**Given** a page number beyond the available results,\n**When** the search is executed,\n**Then** the response is 200 with an empty array.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-003-05 AC2.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-page-beyond` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`, `API-SEARCH-909642`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-011 Invalid pagination parameters\n\n**Given** a negative or non-numeric `page` or `limit`,\n**When** the request is made,\n**Then** the response is 400 with a validation error.\n**And** `page` or `limit` of zero (`ASM-BE-411`), and `limit` above the maximum page size (`ASM-BE-414`), give the same response.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-003-05 AC3; BR-011.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-paging-invalid-400` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`, `API-SEARCH-909642`.\n- **Status.** `DRAFT-UNVERIFIED`: `ASM-BE-411`, `ASM-BE-414`, `Q-BE-401`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-012 Bounded result sets\n\n**Given** a request that omits `page` and `limit`,\n**When** it is executed,\n**Then** a default page size is applied. No response ever exceeds the maximum page size.\n\n- **Traces to.** BR-011 (\"API enforces default/max page size\"); NFR-007.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-default-page-size` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`, `API-SEARCH-909642`.\n- **Status.** `DRAFT-UNVERIFIED`. The default size is not stated (`GAP-BE-424`). The maximum is `ASM-BE-414`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-013 Archived notes excluded by default\n\n**Given** archived and active notes that match,\n**When** the request omits `include_archived`,\n**Then** only active notes are returned.\n\n- **Traces to.** BR-009; US-003-06 AC1.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-archived-default` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`, `API-SEARCH-909642` (`ASM-BE-412`).\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-014 Archived notes on request\n\n**Given** `include_archived=true`,\n**When** the search is executed,\n**Then** archived notes are included in the results.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-003-06 AC2; BR-009 exception.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-include-archived` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`, `API-SEARCH-909642` (`ASM-BE-412`).\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-015 Invalid `include_archived`\n\n**Given** an `include_archived` value that is not valid,\n**When** the request is made,\n**Then** the response is 400 with an error message.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-003-06 AC3.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-include-archived-invalid-400` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`, `API-SEARCH-909642`.\n- **Status.** `DRAFT-UNVERIFIED`. Only `true` and `false` are valid (`ASM-BE-410`).\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-016 Pinned-first over the whole result set\n\n**Given** matches that include pinned and unpinned notes,\n**When** any page is requested,\n**Then** the ordering is applied before the page is cut. Every pinned match precedes every unpinned match across all pages.\n\n- **Traces to.** BR-019; US-003-01 AC1; US-003-05 AC1; `INV-BE-001`; `INV-BE-111`.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-pinned-first-across-pages` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`, `API-SEARCH-909642`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-017 Deterministic order for stable pages\n\n**Given** two notes equal on `pinned` and equal in the within-group order,\n**When** results are ordered,\n**Then** they are ordered by `id` ascending, so pages never overlap or skip a note.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-003-05 AC1 (\"the correct subset\"); derived, see `ASM-BE-408`.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-stable-order` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`, `API-SEARCH-909642`.\n- **Status.** `DRAFT-UNVERIFIED`. The within-group order itself is `GAP-BE-310`, `GAP-BE-426`, `Q-BE-404`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-018 Search latency\n\n**Given** a load of 50 requests per second, or a dataset of about 100k notes, or page sizes up to 100 results,\n**When** a search is executed,\n**Then** p95 latency is under 300 ms, using indexed lookup.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-003-03 AC1, AC2; NFR-006; NFR-007.\n- **Verification.** `perf-search-p95-50rps-100k-notes`, `perf-search-p95-limit-100` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n- **Status.** `DRAFT-UNVERIFIED`: test conditions are `GAP-BE-432`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-019 Search index unavailable\n\n**Given** the search index is degraded or unavailable,\n**When** a search is requested,\n**Then** the response is 503 `SEARCH_UNAVAILABLE` with a retry-after header, instead of hanging.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-003-03 AC3.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-index-down-503` (planned, with fault injection).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n- **Status.** `DRAFT-UNVERIFIED`. The header value and the time bound for \"instead of hanging\" are `GAP-BE-427`. How the fault is detected is `GAP-BE-422`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-020 Note list\n\n**Given** an authenticated caller,\n**When** `GET /notes` is called,\n**Then** the response is 200 with notes, paginated, pinned first and archived excluded unless `include_archived=true`. The operation accepts no `q` and no `tags` (`ASM-BE-409`).\n\n- **Traces to.** BR-019; BR-011; BR-009 (by `ASM-BE-412`). The consumers are US-006-01 and US-006-05 AC1, AC2.\n- **Verification.** `test-list-default`, `test-list-paging`, `test-list-include-archived` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-909642`.\n- **Status.** `DRAFT-UNVERIFIED`: `GAP-BE-428`, `ASM-BE-409`, `ASM-BE-412`, `ASM-BE-416`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-021 Authentication required\n\n**Given** a request without a valid credential,\n**When** either operation is called,\n**Then** the response is 401 `UNAUTHENTICATED` and no note data is returned.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-004-03 (401/403 documented where authentication applies); `backend.auth-model` (derived refusal of unauthenticated callers on note and search rows).\n- **Verification.** `test-search-unauthenticated-401`, `test-list-unauthenticated-401` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`, `API-SEARCH-909642`.\n- **Status.** `DRAFT-UNVERIFIED`. The credential form is `Q-BE-012` and the existing method is `GAP-BE-085`.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-022 Results limited to notes the caller may read\n\n**Given** an authenticated caller,\n**When** either operation is executed,\n**Then** only notes the caller is permitted to read appear in the results and in the total count.\n\n- **Traces to.** `backend.auth-model` ownership predicate.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-visibility` (planned, after the rule is decided).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`, `API-SEARCH-909642`.\n- **Status.** `BLOCKED` on `CON-BE-003` and `Q-BE-003`. The shared-workspace default `Q-BE-001` is a placeholder only.\n\n### BE-SEARCH-FR-023 Query values are data\n\n**Given** `q` or `tags` containing characters that carry meaning to the query engine,\n**When** a search is executed,\n**Then** the values are validated and treated purely as data. They never alter the query.\n\n- **Traces to.** NFR-012.\n- **Verification.** `test-search-injection-q-tags` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-SEARCH-580505`.\n\n### Story trace\n\n| Story | Requirements |\n|---|---|\n| US-003-01 | FR-001, FR-002, FR-003, FR-016 |\n| US-003-02 | FR-002, FR-004, FR-005, FR-006 |\n| US-003-03 | FR-018, FR-019 |\n| US-003-04 | FR-002, FR-007, FR-008 |\n| US-003-05 | FR-009, FR-010, FR-011, FR-012, FR-017 |\n| US-003-06 | FR-013, FR-014, FR-015 |\n\nAll six owned stories are covered. FR-020 to FR-023 carry no US-003 story. FR-020 traces to rules and operation records, FR-021 and FR-022 to the auth model, FR-023 to NFR-012.\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-SEARCH-909642` | GET | `/notes` | `search` | 200 | 400, 401, 403 | List notes, paginated, with pinned notes first and archived notes excluded by default |\n| `API-SEARCH-580505` | GET | `/notes/search` | `search` | 200 | 400, 401, 403, 503 | Keyword and tag search over notes, paginated, with pinned notes first and archived notes excluded by default |\n\n### `API-SEARCH-909642` \u2014 GET `/notes`\n\nList notes, paginated, with pinned notes first and archived notes excluded by default\n\n**Request:** \n- `page` (integer): Page number of the paginated result. A bad value returns 400.\n- `limit` (integer): Page size. The spec gives a default and a maximum but states only 100 as a tested size. A bad value returns 400.\n- `include_archived` (boolean): When true, archived notes are included. By default archived notes are excluded. A bad value returns 400.\n- `tag` (string): Restrict the list to notes carrying this tag. A malformed filter returns 400.\n**Response:** \n- `items` (array, required): The notes on this page. Every pinned note comes before every unpinned note. Order within each group is undefined (GAP-BE-310). Each item shows id, title, body, status (active or archived), pinned, tags, created_at and updated_at.\n- `page` (integer, required): The page number returned. The field name is proposed, because no input names the pagination fields.\n- `limit` (integer, required): The page size applied. The field name is proposed.\n- `total` (integer): Total number of matching notes. The field name is proposed, and the inputs do not say whether a total is returned.\n\n### `API-SEARCH-580505` \u2014 GET `/notes/search`\n\nKeyword and tag search over notes, paginated, with pinned notes first and archived notes excluded by default\n\n**Request:** \n- `q` (string, required): Keyword query matched against notes. A missing q returns 400.\n- `tag` (string): Restrict results to notes carrying this tag. A malformed filter returns 400.\n- `include_archived` (boolean): When true, archived notes are included. By default archived notes are excluded. A bad value returns 400.\n- `page` (integer): Page number of the paginated result. A bad value returns 400.\n- `limit` (integer): Page size, tested up to 100 results. A bad value returns 400.\n**Response:** \n- `items` (array, required): The matching notes on this page. Every pinned note comes before every unpinned note. Order within each group is undefined (GAP-BE-310). Each item shows id, title, body, status, pinned, tags, created_at and updated_at.\n- `page` (integer, required): The page number returned. The field name is proposed.\n- `limit` (integer, required): The page size applied. The field name is proposed.\n- `total` (integer): Total number of matching notes. The field name is proposed, and the inputs do not say whether a total is returned.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.invariants | status: DERIVED -->\n## 6. Invariants\n\nThese hold after every operation of this module. Sources are cited or the derivation is stated.\n\n- **INV-BE-110.** Neither operation changes any stored data. [backend.module-map: \"Read-only query\"]\n- **INV-BE-111.** The ordering is fixed before slicing. For any query, no note on page *p* is unpinned while a pinned note matching the same query appears on a later page. This is the operational form of `INV-BE-001`. [requirements.md BR-019]\n- **INV-BE-112.** Unless `include_archived` is `true`, no returned note has status `archived`. [requirements.md BR-009]\n- **INV-BE-113.** No response contains more notes than the maximum page size. [requirements.md BR-011; value `GAP-BE-424`]\n- **INV-BE-114.** A 400, 401, 403 or 503 response carries no note data. Derived from the error envelope in `backend.error-model`, which has no field for note content.\n- **INV-BE-115.** Every returned note satisfies every supplied criterion. A note must match `q` and the tag filter together when both are supplied. [stories.md US-003-04 AC1]\n- **INV-BE-116.** The total count equals the size of the filtered set before slicing. It is not less than the number of items on the page. Derived from \"the correct subset is returned along with a total count\". [stories.md US-003-05 AC1]\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.authorization | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 7. Authorization\n\nThe rows below repeat the project permission matrix for this module's two operations. [backend.auth-model] A role has no permission an input does not grant (`ASM-BE-302`).\n\n| Operation | Unauthenticated caller | Team member | Platform/DevOps, Release/CI owner, Auditor, Eng. Lead |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-SEARCH-909642` (note list) | D* (401) | `B` (`CON-BE-003`) | - |\n| `API-SEARCH-580505` (note search) | D* (401) | `B` (`CON-BE-003`) | - |\n\n`D*` is a refusal derived from the stories, not cited. `B` is blocked on the named item.\n\n**BLOCKED subpart: the ownership predicate.** Which notes a team member may see in a list or search result depends on `Q-BE-003` and `CON-BE-003` (Critical). The placeholder default `Q-BE-001` says any authenticated team member may read any note. It is not applied as a rule here. US-006-01 speaks of \"my active notes\", which is a signal that the answer may be user-scoped. That signal is not a decision.\n\n**No further restriction on archived content.** Any caller who may search may pass `include_archived`. [requirements.md BR-009: \"Caller may pass include_archived=true\"]\n\n**Refusals.** An unauthenticated caller gets 401 `UNAUTHENTICATED`. An authenticated caller who is not permitted gets 403 `FORBIDDEN`, and the rule for that is `BLOCKED`. The credential form is `Q-BE-012` and the existing method is `GAP-BE-085`.\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.jobs | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 8. Jobs\n\n- **Scheduled work: none stated.** No input requires a sweep or schedule from this module.\n- **Index maintenance.** If the search index is separate from the note tables, something must keep it in step with note writes and with any deletion allowed by NFR-019. No input defines it. See `GAP-BE-422`.\n- **Idempotency.** Both operations are `GET` and change no data (`INV-BE-110`). No idempotency key applies.\n- **Retries.** No server-side retry is stated. A caller that receives 503 is expected to wait for the retry-after interval before retrying. [stories.md US-003-03 AC3]\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 9. Test plan\n\nAll test names are planned. Test data uses synthetic text and no real-looking PII.\n\n**Unit**\n\n- `test-search-param-validation-q-tags` (FR-003, FR-006, FR-008)\n- `test-search-param-validation-paging` (FR-011)\n- `test-search-param-validation-include-archived` (FR-015)\n- `test-search-ordering-comparator` (FR-016, FR-017)\n- `test-search-tag-combine` (FR-005)\n\n**Integration** (PostgreSQL 16 [platform.md])\n\n- `test-search-keyword-match`\n- `test-search-empty-result`\n- `test-search-tags-only-ok`\n- `test-search-tag-filter`\n- `test-search-combined`\n- `test-search-pagination-subset`\n- `test-search-page-beyond`\n- `test-search-archived-default`\n- `test-search-include-archived`\n- `test-search-pinned-first-across-pages`\n- `test-search-stable-order`\n- `test-list-default`\n- `test-list-paging`\n- `test-list-include-archived`\n- `test-search-unauthenticated-401`\n- `test-list-unauthenticated-401`\n- `test-search-index-down-503` (fault injection)\n- `test-search-visibility` (after `CON-BE-003` is decided)\n\n**Contract**\n\n- Both operations' responses conform to the contract DevAgent renders. [requirements.md NFR-008]\n- Error responses use the envelope in `backend.error-model`.\n- The spec for `API-SEARCH-580505` carries an example request, an example response and an example empty-result response. [stories.md US-004-05; requirements.md NFR-009] `api-docs` owns publication.\n\n**Performance**\n\n- `perf-search-p95-50rps-100k-notes` and `perf-search-p95-limit-100`. [stories.md US-003-03; requirements.md NFR-006, NFR-007] The test conditions are `GAP-BE-432`.\n\n**Security**\n\n- `test-search-injection-q-tags`. [requirements.md NFR-012]\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.search.gaps | status: DERIVED -->\n## 10. Gaps, contradictions, assumptions and questions\n\nItems from the project spec are cited by id and not redefined here: `CON-BE-003`, `CON-BE-300`, `Q-BE-001`, `Q-BE-003`, `Q-BE-012`, `GAP-BE-035`, `GAP-BE-085`, `GAP-BE-104`, `GAP-BE-310`, `ASM-BE-302`.\n\n### Contradiction\n\n- **`CON-BE-401`** (non-critical). Two acceptance criteria disagree about `GET /notes/search` without `q`.\n  - Claim A: \"Given the q query parameter is missing or empty \u2026 API returns 400\". [stories.md US-003-01 AC3]\n  - Claim B: \"GET /notes/search?tags=project-x is called, then only notes with that tag are returned\". There is no `q`. [stories.md US-003-02 AC1]\n  - Consequence of A: tag-only requests fail, US-003-02 AC1 cannot be met as written, and tag filtering needs a keyword.\n  - Consequence of B: `q` is optional, the \"missing q gives 400\" test fails, and the endpoint doubles as a tag listing.\n  - Severity: non-critical under core rule 8. It changes request validation only. It does not change a stored data shape, a state machine, an authorisation rule or a money/PII path.\n  - Reading used: 400 when `q` and `tags` are both absent, or `q` is present but empty. A request with `tags` and no `q` is a tag-only search (`BE-SEARCH-FR-003`). Nothing is decided; `Q-BE-403` carries the default.\n\n### Contradiction checks run\n\n- **Story to design.** `design-brief.md` holds acceptance-criteria text for EP-001 and US-002-01 only. Nothing in it concerns US-003, so there was nothing to compare.\n- **Story to requirement.** BR-009 to BR-011 and BR-019 agree with US-003-01 to US-003-06. Nothing new.\n- **Requirement to requirement.** BR-019 (\"ordering \u2026 unchanged\") and US-003-01 (\"ordered by relevance\") are consistent for search. The list operation's order is undefined, which is a gap and not a contradiction (`GAP-BE-310`, `GAP-BE-426`).\n- **Story to story.** `CON-BE-401` above. No other conflict found.\n- **Design to design.** Not applicable.\n\nNo other contradiction was found. `CON-BE-003` and `CON-BE-300` still touch this module through visibility and through note existence.\n\n### Gaps\n\n- **`GAP-BE-422`**: the search index. Missing: whether it is separate from the note tables, how it stays in step with note writes and deletions, whether a note is searchable immediately after it is written, and how degradation is detected. Degrades: data-model, state-machine, jobs, `BE-SEARCH-FR-018` and `BE-SEARCH-FR-019`. Instead: indexes proposed under `ASM-BE-417`, with the fault state described only by its response. Closing artefact: a platform decision on the search index.\n- **`GAP-BE-423`**: the keyword matching rule (case, whole word or substring, stemming). Degrades `BE-SEARCH-FR-001`. Instead: the requirement is written independent of the rule. Closing artefact: a matching rule from the product owner.\n- **`GAP-BE-424`**: the default page size, the maximum page size, and the behaviour above the maximum (clamp or reject). BR-011 requires them and NFR-007 mentions 100. Degrades `FR-011`, `FR-012`, `INV-BE-113`. Instead: `ASM-BE-414`. Closing artefact: page-size values and the over-limit rule.\n- **`GAP-BE-425`**: how several tags are written in `tags`, whether tag matching is case-sensitive, and the tag name format (see `GAP-BE-035`). Degrades `FR-005`, `FR-006`. Closing artefact: a tag name rule and a parameter format.\n- **`GAP-BE-426`**: the definition of \"relevance\", and the within-group order for the list operation. Extends `GAP-BE-310`. Degrades `FR-001`, `FR-017`, `FR-020`. Instead: id tie-break only (`ASM-BE-408`). Closing artefact: an ordering rule from the product owner.\n- **`GAP-BE-427`**: the retry-after value, and the time within which the 503 must be returned. Degrades `FR-019`. Closing artefact: a value and a time bound.\n- **`GAP-BE-428`**: no input states the list operation's parameters or failure statuses. Only the operation record, BR-019, and the web-app stories describe it. Degrades `FR-020`. Instead: `ASM-BE-409`, `ASM-BE-412`, `ASM-BE-416`. Closing artefact: the list operation's operation record fields.\n- **`GAP-BE-429`**: validation of `q`: maximum length, and whether whitespace-only counts as empty. Complements `GAP-BE-035`. Degrades `FR-003`. Closing artefact: field-level requirements.\n- **`GAP-BE-430`**: what each result item contains (full note or a summary). US-006-01 needs at least the title and the archived/active indicator. Closing artefact: the response fields in the operation records.\n- **`GAP-BE-431`**: what a \"malformed filter combination\" is, and whether unknown query parameters are ignored or refused. `FR-008` is `BLOCKED` on it. Closing artefact: a rule for combinations.\n- **`GAP-BE-432`**: the performance test conditions: environment, dataset content, measurement window and percentile method. Degrades `FR-018`. Closing artefact: a test specification.\n- **`GAP-BE-433`**: whether the text of `q` and `tags` may appear in logs. NFR-018 covers email addresses and login codes only, and is itself corrupted (`GAP-BE-104`). Closing artefact: a logging policy.\n\n### Assumptions\n\n- **`ASM-BE-406`**: search owns no tables and reads the notes tables. Reason: the module map says read-only. Confirmed when `notes` and `search` boundaries are approved.\n- **`ASM-BE-407`**: \"content\" in US-003-01 means the note `body`. Reason: the Note entity has title and body only. Confirmed by the product owner.\n- **`ASM-BE-408`**: the last ordering key is `id` ascending. Reason: stable pagination needs a total order. Confirmed by `Q-BE-404`.\n- **`ASM-BE-409`**: the list operation uses `page`, `limit` and `include_archived`, and no `q` or `tags`. Reason: its operation record describes pagination and archived exclusion only. Confirmed by `GAP-BE-428`'s closing artefact.\n- **`ASM-BE-410`**: `include_archived` accepts exactly `true` and `false`. Reason: US-003-06 cites `true` and an invalid-value case. Confirmed by the product owner.\n- **`ASM-BE-411`**: page numbering starts at 1, and a `page` or `limit` of 0 is invalid. Reason: negative values are invalid (US-003-05 AC3) and zero has no meaning for either. Confirmed by `GAP-BE-424`'s closing artefact.\n- **`ASM-BE-412`**: the list operation applies archived exclusion by default, `include_archived`, pinned-first order and the page-size bounds as search does.
