Reason: its operation record summary, BR-019 (\"search and list results\"), BR-011, and US-006-05 (\"default notes list view\"). Confirmed by `GAP-BE-428`'s closing artefact.\n- **`ASM-BE-413`**: several tags combine as AND. Reason: \"narrow results\". Confirmed by `Q-BE-402`.\n- **`ASM-BE-414`**: the maximum page size is 100, and a larger `limit` gets 400. Reason: NFR-007 cites page sizes up to 100, and invalid pagination already gives 400. Confirmed by `Q-BE-401`.\n- **`ASM-BE-415`**: checks run in the order authenticate, authorise, validate, check index availability. Reason: no input states it. Confirmed by the product owner.\n- **`ASM-BE-416`**: the list operation does not read the search index and never returns 503. Reason: the approved revision's operation records list only 401 and 403 errors for it. [feedback.md, approved BACKEND-SPEC.md api-surface] Confirmed by `GAP-BE-422`'s closing artefact.\n- **`ASM-BE-417`**: the indexes in the data model are proposals: NOTE on (`status`, `pinned`, `id`) and NOTE_TAG on (`tag_name`, `note_id`). Reason: they serve the queries stated. Confirmed by the final ordering rule (`GAP-BE-426`).\n\n### Questions\n\nUnattended run: each takes its stated default silently. None was asked of the human.\n\n| ID | Class | Question | Default taken |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `Q-BE-401` | degradable | What are the default and maximum page sizes, and is a `limit` above the maximum rejected or clamped? | maximum 100, above-maximum rejected with 400 (`ASM-BE-414`). Default size unasserted (`GAP-BE-424`). |\n| `Q-BE-402` | degradable | Do several tags combine as AND or OR? | AND (`ASM-BE-413`) |\n| `Q-BE-403` | degradable | Is a search with `tags` and no `q` valid? | yes (`CON-BE-401` reading) |\n| `Q-BE-404` | degradable | What is the order within the pinned and unpinned groups, and how is relevance defined? | search by relevance, as cited, with relevance undefined; id tie-break only (`ASM-BE-408`) |\n\nOpen questions from the project spec that affect this module: `Q-BE-003` (blocking, visibility) and `Q-BE-012` (blocking, credential form).\n\n### Needs from other lanes\n\nStated here as plain requirements. Handoff records are created from them elsewhere.\n\n- **Web-app:** the search box must handle 400 for invalid parameters, 503 with a retry-after header, and the empty-result 200. [stories.md US-006-03]\n- **Notes module (same surface):** the index requirements in the data model must be created in the `notes` migrations, or a separate index structure decided.\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.search.gaps -->"}, {"id": "backend/modules/residency-compliance-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/residency-compliance-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.frontmatter | status: DERIVED -->\n```yaml\nid: residency-compliance\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: [CON-BE-151, Q-BE-152, Q-BE-151, GAP-BE-305]\nscope: [API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-731179, API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-241176, API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-019122, API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-913995, API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094, API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056, API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792]\n```\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.purpose | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 1. Purpose\n\n**Responsibility.** The `residency-compliance` module decides whether notes_api infrastructure sits in approved EU regions, and it records that decision. It owns the INFRA_RESOURCE and DRIFT_ALERT entities from the project domain model. It also owns two supporting entities this module introduces, the approved-region list and the scan run (`ASM-BE-456`). [backend.domain-model, backend.module-map; requirements.md BR-001 to BR-004, NFR-001 to NFR-004]\n\n1. **Provisioning validation.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-731179` returns a verdict on whether a database or compute provisioning request targets an approved EU region. [stories.md US-001-01]\n2. **Backup destination validation.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-241176` returns a verdict on whether a backup destination is in an approved EU region. A non-EU destination is refused within 30 seconds, and the module writes nothing. [stories.md US-001-02; design-brief.md AC-001-02-02]\n3. **CI/CD residency gate.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-019122` evaluates the regions of the components in a deployment. A non-EU region, or region metadata that is missing or unreadable, blocks the deployment. [stories.md US-001-03; requirements.md BR-002, NFR-002]\n4. **Compliance report.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-913995` lists every component with its region and a `pass`, `fail` or `unverified` status, plus the percentage EU-verified. [stories.md US-001-04; requirements.md NFR-004]\n5. **Launch-gate status.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094` derives the gate status from a freshly refreshed report, for Engineering Lead review. [stories.md US-001-05; requirements.md BR-003]\n6. **Drift scanning and alerting.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056` runs a scan on demand. The same scan runs periodically (section 9). A resource found in a non-EU region raises a drift alert to the DevOps team. `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792` lists the alerts. [stories.md US-001-06; requirements.md BR-004, NFR-003]\n\n**Stories owned.** US-001-01 to US-001-06 map one-to-one to the operations above, in order.\n\n**Not responsible for.**\n\n- **Creating or changing infrastructure.** This module never provisions a resource, writes a backup, or runs a deployment. It returns a verdict. The region constraints in infrastructure code and the pipeline step that acts on the verdict are outside the module. [requirements.md BR-001 Enforcement] See `GAP-BE-475`.\n- **Backup scheduling and retention.** The backup job and its frequency are infrastructure concerns (`GAP-BE-307`). The module validates the destination only.\n- **Remediation.** No input says what happens to a drifted resource. The module reports it and does not move or stop it.\n- **Release authorisation.** Whether a passed gate authorises release, or the Engineering Lead signs off, is open (`CON-BE-151`, `Q-BE-152`). The module derives gate status and does not decide release.\n- **The alert channel and recipients** (`GAP-BE-308`). The module raises the alert and hands it to a notifier.\n- **Notes, search, sign-in, the OpenAPI document and the scope guardrail.** Other modules own these. [backend.module-map]\n- **Application logging policy and encryption at rest** (`GAP-BE-304`). No claim about either is made here.\n\n**Identifier note.** New identifiers come from the block reserved for this module: `ASM-BE-456` to `ASM-BE-470`, `CON-BE-451`, `GAP-BE-472` to `GAP-BE-484`, `INV-BE-160` to `INV-BE-166`, `Q-BE-451` and `Q-BE-452`. Identifiers below those numbers are cited from the project spec.\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 2. Data model\n\nThe database is PostgreSQL 16. [platform.md] No input states column types, nullability or constraints for this module. Whether residency data lives in the application database at all is open (`GAP-BE-305`). The definitions below are proposals under `ASM-BE-456` and `ASM-BE-466`. Column names for INFRA_RESOURCE and DRIFT_ALERT come from the project domain model. [backend.domain-model]\n\n**approved_region** (supporting)\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint and notes |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `region` | text | NOT NULL | none | primary key. Contents are not supplied (`Q-BE-151`), so none are written here. |\n| `added_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | set at insert | |\n\nIndex: the primary key, which serves the membership lookup `region = $1`. Who may change this list is `GAP-BE-477`.\n\n**scan_run** (supporting)\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint and notes |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `scan_run_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | assigned by the application | primary key |\n| `trigger` | text | NOT NULL | none | `CHECK (trigger IN ('periodic','on-demand','gate-refresh'))` |\n| `state` | text | NOT NULL | `'running'` | `CHECK (state IN ('running','completed','failed'))` |\n| `started_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | |\n| `finished_at` | timestamptz | NULL | none | `CHECK ((state = 'running') = (finished_at IS NULL))` and `CHECK (finished_at IS NULL OR finished_at >= started_at)` |\n| `failure_reason` | text | NULL | none | set only when `state = 'failed'`. It never contains a credential. |\n\nIndexes:\n- A partial unique index admitting at most one row with `state = 'running'`. It enforces `INV-BE-166`.\n- A btree on `(started_at DESC)` restricted to `state = 'completed'`. It serves the query \"latest completed scan\" for the report (`ASM-BE-468`).\n\n**infra_resource**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint and notes |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `resource_id` | text | NOT NULL | none | primary key. The identifier format is provider-defined and unstated. |\n| `resource_type` | text | NOT NULL | none | no `CHECK`: an unrecognised type must be storable so it can be flagged `unverified`, not omitted. [design-brief.md AC-001-04-02; `ASM-BE-457`] |\n| `region` | text | NULL | none | NULL means missing or unreadable. `CHECK (region IS NULL OR region <> '')` |\n| `compliance_status` | text | NOT NULL | none | `CHECK (compliance_status IN ('pass','fail','unverified'))` |\n| `last_scanned_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | rows are created only by a scan |\n| `last_seen_scan_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | none | foreign key to `scan_run`, `ON DELETE RESTRICT` |\n\nIndex: btree on `(last_seen_scan_id, compliance_status)`. It serves the report query (all rows for one scan) and the per-status counts behind the percentage. The foreign key is `RESTRICT` because no deletion is in scope (`GAP-BE-480`).\n\n**drift_alert**\n\n| Column | Type | Null | Default | Constraint and notes |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `id` | uuid | NOT NULL | assigned by the application | primary key |\n| `resource_id` | text | NOT NULL | none | foreign key to `infra_resource`, `ON DELETE RESTRICT` |\n| `scan_run_id` | uuid | NOT NULL | none | foreign key to `scan_run`, `ON DELETE RESTRICT` |\n| `detected_region` | text | NOT NULL | none | `CHECK (detected_region <> '')`. An alert exists only when a region was readable (`Q-BE-451`). |\n| `raised_at` | timestamptz | NOT NULL | none | |\n| `delivered_at` | timestamptz | NULL | none | `CHECK (delivered_at IS NULL OR delivered_at >= raised_at)` (`ASM-BE-470`) |\n\nIndexes:\n- `UNIQUE (resource_id, scan_run_id)`. It enforces `INV-BE-165` and makes a retried alert step idempotent.\n- A btree on `(raised_at DESC)`. It serves `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792`, newest first (`GAP-BE-484`).\n\n**Time.** All instants are `timestamptz`, stored as UTC instants. Time-zone handling is otherwise unstated (`ASM-BE-466`).\n\n**PII.** The tables above hold no personal data: resource identifiers, region names and timestamps only. This is a statement about the design and not about handling policy. Encryption at rest and retention are unstated (`GAP-BE-304`, `GAP-BE-480`), and no claim is made for either.\n\n**Migration placement.** The project migration plan lists only `infra_resources` and `drift_alerts` under order 007, and marks it `BLOCKED` on `GAP-BE-305`. `approved_region` and `scan_run` must precede them. `GAP-BE-482`.\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 3. State machine\n\nFour things carry state or a derived status: a scan run, a resource's compliance status, the launch gate, and a drift alert. Validation and gate-check operations are stateless verdicts and have no persisted state (`ASM-BE-461`).\n\n### 3.1 scan_run\n\n**States.** `running`, `completed`, `failed`. Terminal states are `completed` and `failed`.\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard | Effect |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| none | `running` | periodic tick, `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056`, or a launch-gate refresh via `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094` | no other scan is `running` (`ASM-BE-464`) | row created with its trigger |\n| `running` | `completed` | every inventory item evaluated and persisted | none | `finished_at` set. Alerts are raised (3.4). |\n| `running` | `failed` | the inventory could not be obtained or evaluated | none | `finished_at` and `failure_reason` set. No alert is raised for the failure (`Q-BE-452`). |\n\n**Refused transition.** A start request while a scan is `running` is refused, and the running scan is unaffected. The response status is not stated (`GAP-BE-473`). A `running` scan that never finishes has no stated timeout (`GAP-BE-481`).\n\n### 3.2 Compliance status of a resource\n\nThe status is recomputed on every scan, so no state is terminal. Rules are applied in this order (`ASM-BE-458`; `DRAFT-UNVERIFIED` because no input states the precedence):\n\n| Order | Condition | Status |\n|---|---|---|\n| 1 | region readable and not in `approved_region` | `fail` (any resource type) |\n| 2 | `resource_type` not recognised (`ASM-BE-457`) | `unverified` |\n| 3 | region missing or unreadable (recognised type) | `fail` |\n| 4 | otherwise (region in `approved_region`, recognised type) | `pass` |\n\nSources:\n- Rule 1: [requirements.md BR-001, BR-004].\n- Rule 2: [design-brief.md AC-001-04-02].\n- Rule 3: [design-brief.md AC-001-03-03; `INV-BE-007`].\n\nRule 1 precedes rule 2 so that an unrecognised resource found outside the EU still raises an alert (BR-004 says \"any running resource\").\n\n**Gate-check verdict** (stateless, `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-019122`). A deployment is compliant only if every supplied component has a readable region in `approved_region`. The resource type is not considered (`ASM-BE-467`). An empty component list is not compliant (`ASM-BE-462`).\n\n### 3.3 Launch gate\n\nThe status is derived on each call. It is never stored, so it has no terminal state.\n\n| Status | Derived when |\n|---|---|\n| `PASSED` | a scan run by this request completed, the report has at least one row, and every row is `pass` (`INV-BE-163`, `INV-BE-164`) |\n| `FAILED` | otherwise: any `fail` or `unverified` row, no rows, or the refresh scan failed or was refused (`ASM-BE-460`) |\n\n**BLOCKED subpart.** What `PASSED` causes is unresolved: \"release is authorized\" in AC-001-05-01, against Engineering Lead sign-off in BR-003. If sign-off is required, a recorded sign-off state is needed, with its own operation. `CON-BE-151`, `Q-BE-152`.\n\n### 3.4 DRIFT_ALERT\n\n**States.** `PENDING-DELIVERY` (`delivered_at` is NULL) and `DELIVERED` (`delivered_at` set). The terminal state is `DELIVERED`.\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard | Effect |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| none | `PENDING-DELIVERY` | scan completes with a resource `fail` on a readable non-EU region | no row for this (resource, scan) pair | row created, then handed to the notifier (`ASM-BE-463`) |\n| `PENDING-DELIVERY` | `PENDING-DELIVERY` | notifier did not acknowledge | retry limit not stated (`GAP-BE-479`) | retry |\n| `PENDING-DELIVERY` | `DELIVERED` | notifier acknowledged | none | `delivered_at` set |\n\nNo resolved or acknowledged-by-human state exists in any input (`GAP-BE-479`).\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 4. Requirements\n\n\"Verdict\" means the operation's compliant or refused outcome. Its shape and statuses are in the contract rendered from the operation records. Statuses for refusals are not stated by any input (`GAP-BE-473`).\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-001 Accept a compliant provisioning request\n\n**Given** a request to provision a database or compute resource whose target region is in `approved_region`,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-731179` is called,\n**Then** a compliant verdict is returned, and the module creates no INFRA_RESOURCE row (`INV-BE-161`).\n\n- **Traces to.** US-001-01; AC-001-01-01; BR-001; NFR-001.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-provisioning-eu-accepted` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-731179`.\n- **Note.** The region list contents are `Q-BE-151`. The post-creation check that both resources report an approved region is made by a scan (FR-017).\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-002 Refuse a non-EU provisioning request\n\n**Given** a provisioning request whose target region is not in `approved_region`,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-731179` is called,\n**Then** a refusal verdict is returned and nothing is recorded as `pass`. The caller must not create the resource (`ASM-BE-461`, `GAP-BE-475`).\n\n- **Traces to.** US-001-01; AC-001-01-02; BR-001.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-provisioning-non-eu-refused` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-731179`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-003 Refuse a provisioning request with no determinable region\n\n**Given** a provisioning request whose target region is absent or unreadable,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-731179` is called,\n**Then** a refusal verdict is returned. The request is never treated as compliant by default.\n\n- **Traces to.** Derived from AC-001-03-03 and `INV-BE-007`, applied to provisioning. No input states this case for provisioning. Status `DRAFT-UNVERIFIED`.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-provisioning-missing-region-refused` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-731179`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-004 Accept a compliant backup destination\n\n**Given** a backup destination in `approved_region`,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-241176` is called,\n**Then** a compliant verdict is returned.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-001-02; AC-001-02-01; BR-001.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-backup-eu-accepted` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-241176`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-005 Refuse a non-EU backup destination within 30 seconds\n\n**Given** a backup destination configuration pointing outside the EU,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-241176` is called before the backup runs,\n**Then** a refusal verdict naming a residency violation is returned within 30 seconds of request receipt, and the module writes no backup data (`INV-BE-161`). The calling pipeline fails the job on this verdict (`GAP-BE-475`).\n\n- **Traces to.** US-001-02; AC-001-02-02; BR-001.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-backup-non-eu-refused-within-30s` (planned, with clock injection).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-241176`.\n- **Note.** How the 30 seconds is shared between pipeline and operation is unstated; the operation is held to the whole budget (`ASM-BE-469`). The error code name is a proposal (`ASM-BE-301`; `GAP-BE-473`).\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-006 Refuse a backup destination with no determinable region\n\n**Given** a backup destination whose region is absent or unreadable,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-241176` is called,\n**Then** a refusal verdict is returned.\n\n- **Traces to.** Derived from AC-001-03-03 and `INV-BE-007`. `DRAFT-UNVERIFIED`.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-backup-missing-region-refused` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-241176`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-007 Gate check passes when all components are in the EU\n\n**Given** a deployment whose components all have readable regions in `approved_region`,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-019122` is called,\n**Then** a passing verdict is returned and the deployment may proceed.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-001-03; AC-001-03-01; BR-002; NFR-002.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-gate-all-eu-passes` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-019122`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-008 Gate check blocks a non-EU component\n\n**Given** a deployment in which at least one component's region is not in `approved_region`,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-019122` is called,\n**Then** a blocking verdict is returned, and the pipeline fails the build.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-001-03; AC-001-03-02; BR-002; NFR-002.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-gate-non-eu-blocks` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-019122`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-009 Gate check fails on unreadable region metadata\n\n**Given** a deployment in which a component's region metadata is missing or unreadable,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-019122` is called,\n**Then** a blocking verdict is returned, never a pass by default (`INV-BE-007`).\n\n- **Traces to.** US-001-03; AC-001-03-03; `INV-BE-007`.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-gate-unreadable-region-blocks` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-019122`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-010 Gate check on an empty component list blocks\n\n**Given** a deployment that declares no components, or an empty `approved_region` list,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-019122` is called,\n**Then** a blocking verdict is returned (`ASM-BE-462`).\n\n- **Traces to.** Derived from AC-001-03-03 (fail-closed) and NFR-002. `DRAFT-UNVERIFIED`.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-gate-empty-blocks` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-019122`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-011 Report lists every component\n\n**Given** resources have been scanned,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-913995` is called,\n**Then** the report lists each resource of the latest completed scan, with its region and a `pass`, `fail` or `unverified` status, and an overall percentage compliant (`INV-BE-162`, `INV-BE-163`).\n\n- **Traces to.** US-001-04; AC-001-04-01; NFR-001; NFR-004.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-report-lists-all` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-913995`.\n- **Note.** If no completed scan exists the report is unavailable. The status is `GAP-BE-473`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-012 Report flags unrecognised resource types\n\n**Given** a resource whose type the scanner does not recognise,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-913995` is called,\n**Then** the resource appears with status `unverified`, never omitted.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-001-04; AC-001-04-02.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-report-unrecognised-type-flagged` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-913995`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-013 Percentage metric\n\n**Given** a report with *n* rows of which *p* are `pass`,\n**When** the report is generated,\n**Then** the percentage is *p* / *n* \u00d7 100. `fail` and `unverified` rows count in *n*. If *n* is zero, the report states no resources were evaluated and does not state 100% (`ASM-BE-459`).\n\n- **Traces to.** US-001-04; AC-001-04-01; NFR-001; NFR-004 (\"100% accurate\").\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-report-percentage` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-913995`.\n- **Note.** The report states the time of the scan it is built from (`ASM-BE-460`).\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-014 Launch gate passes at 100%\n\n**Given** a freshly refreshed report in which every resource is `pass` and there is at least one resource,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094` is called,\n**Then** the gate status is `PASSED`.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-001-05; AC-001-05-01; BR-003; NFR-004.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-launch-gate-passed` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094`.\n- **Note.** What `PASSED` authorises is FR-016.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-015 Launch gate fails below 100%\n\n**Given** the refreshed report shows any `fail` or `unverified` row, no rows, or the refresh scan did not complete,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094` is called,\n**Then** the gate status is `FAILED`.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-001-05; AC-001-05-02; BR-003.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-launch-gate-failed` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-016 Effect of a passed gate on release\n\n**Status: BLOCKED.** AC-001-05-01 says a passed gate means \"release is authorized\". BR-003 says the launch gate is enforced by Engineering Lead sign-off. `CON-BE-151` is Critical because it touches an authorisation rule. Unblocked by an answer to `Q-BE-152`. If sign-off is required, a sign-off record and a further operation record are needed.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-001-05; AC-001-05-01; BR-003.\n- **Verification.** (planned) `test-residency-launch-gate-release-effect`, written after `Q-BE-152` is answered.\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-017 Scan evaluates and records resources\n\n**Given** a scan is started by `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056` or by the periodic job,\n**When** it completes,\n**Then** every inventory item is upserted into `infra_resource` with a status per section 3.2, `last_scanned_at` and `last_seen_scan_id` set. When every resource is in an approved region, no alert is raised and the scan logs a pass.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-001-06; AC-001-06-01; AC-001-01-01; BR-004.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-scan-all-eu-no-alert` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056`.\n- **Note.** The inventory source is `GAP-BE-474`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-018 Scan raises a drift alert for a non-EU resource\n\n**Given** a scan finds a running resource with a readable region outside `approved_region`,\n**When** the scan completes,\n**Then** one DRIFT_ALERT is created for that resource and scan, and the DevOps team is alerted with resource details (`INV-BE-165`, `ASM-BE-463`).\n\n- **Traces to.** US-001-06; AC-001-06-02; BR-004; NFR-003.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-scan-non-eu-alerts` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056`.\n- **Note.** An unreadable region raises no alert (`Q-BE-451`). A failed scan raises no alert (`Q-BE-452`).\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-019 Alert timing\n\n**Given** a scan detects drift,\n**When** the scan completes,\n**Then** the alert is dispatched at the end of that scan and in all cases within 15 minutes of detection.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-001-06; AC-001-06-02 (\"immediately\"); NFR-003 (15 minutes). The two wordings are `CON-BE-451`.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-alert-dispatch-latency` (planned, with clock injection).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056`.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-020 List drift alerts\n\n**Given** drift alerts exist,\n**When** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792` is called,\n**Then** they are returned newest first, each with its resource, detected region and raise time.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-001-06; AC-001-06-02.\n- **Verification.** `test-residency-drift-alerts-listed` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792`.\n- **Note.** Pagination, filtering and order are unstated (`GAP-BE-484`). The proposal is newest first.\n\n### BE-RESIDENCY-COMPLIANCE-FR-021 Approved region list\n\n**Status: BLOCKED.** Every verdict above depends on `approved_region`. AC-001-01-01 refers to \"the approved EU region list\", and no input contains it. Unblocked by `Q-BE-151`. How the list is seeded and changed is `GAP-BE-477`.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-001-01; AC-001-01-01; BR-001.\n- **Verification.** (planned) `test-residency-approved-region-seed`.\n- **API.** none.\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\n| id | method | path | module | success | errors | summary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-913995` | GET | `/residency/compliance-report` | `residency-compliance` | 200 | 401, 403, 500 | Generate the compliance report listing every infrastructure component with region and pass, fail or unverified status, and the percentage EU-verified (US-001-04) |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792` | GET | `/residency/drift-alerts` | `residency-compliance` | 200 | 400, 401, 403, 500 | List drift alerts raised for resources found in non-EU regions after launch (US-001-06) |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-019122` | POST | `/residency/gate-checks` | `residency-compliance` | 200 | 400, 401, 403, 500 | CI/CD residency gate: check the regions of components in a deployment; a non-EU region or unreadable region metadata fails the check and blocks the build (US-001-03) |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094` | GET | `/residency/launch-gate` | `residency-compliance` | 200 | 401, 403, 500 | Return launch-gate status derived from the freshly refreshed compliance report, for Engineering Lead review (US-001-05) |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056` | POST | `/residency/scans` | `residency-compliance` | 200 | 401, 403, 500, 503 | Run a post-launch residency drift scan on demand; the same scan runs periodically and alerts the DevOps team on any non-EU resource (US-001-06) |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-241176` | POST | `/residency/validations/backups` | `residency-compliance` | 200 | 400, 401, 403, 422, 500 | Validate that a backup destination is in an approved EU region; a non-EU destination fails within 30 seconds with a residency violation and writes nothing (US-001-02) |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-731179` | POST | `/residency/validations/provisioning` | `residency-compliance` | 200 | 400, 401, 403, 422, 500 | Validate that a database or compute provisioning request targets an approved EU region (US-001-01) |\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-913995` \u2014 GET `/residency/compliance-report`\n\nGenerate the compliance report listing every infrastructure component with region and pass, fail or unverified status, and the percentage EU-verified (US-001-04)\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `generated_at` (string, required): When the report was refreshed\n- `resources` (array, required): Each component with resource type, region, compliance status (pass, fail or unverified) and last scan time\n- `eu_verified_percentage` (number, required): Percentage of infrastructure components verified as EU\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792` \u2014 GET `/residency/drift-alerts`\n\nList drift alerts raised for resources found in non-EU regions after launch (US-001-06)\n\n**Request:** \n- `limit` (integer): Maximum number of alerts to return\n- `page` (integer): Page number of the alert list\n**Response:** \n- `alerts` (array, required): Drift alerts, each with resource id, detected region and raised-at time\n- `total` (integer, required): Total number of alerts\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-019122` \u2014 POST `/residency/gate-checks`\n\nCI/CD residency gate: check the regions of components in a deployment; a non-EU region or unreadable region metadata fails the check and blocks the build (US-001-03)\n\n**Request:** \n- `deployment_id` (string, required): Identifier of the pipeline deployment being checked\n- `components` (array, required): Components in the deployment, each with its resource type and region metadata\n**Response:** \n- `result` (string, required): pass or fail; fail blocks deployment\n- `checked_at` (string, required): Timestamp of the check\n- `violations` (array, required): Components found in non-EU regions or with missing or unreadable region metadata\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094` \u2014 GET `/residency/launch-gate`\n\nReturn launch-gate status derived from the freshly refreshed compliance report, for Engineering Lead review (US-001-05)\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `status` (string, required): Launch gate status: passed or not passed\n- `report_generated_at` (string, required): Timestamp of the compliance report the status is based on\n- `blocking_resources` (array, required): Components that are non-EU or unverified and prevent the gate from passing\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056` \u2014 POST `/residency/scans`\n\nRun a post-launch residency drift scan on demand; the same scan runs periodically and alerts the DevOps team on any non-EU resource (US-001-06)\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `scanned_at` (string, required): Timestamp of the scan\n- `result` (string, required): pass when all resources are in EU regions, otherwise drift\n- `drift_alerts` (array, required): Alerts raised by this scan with resource details; empty when no drift\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-241176` \u2014 POST `/residency/validations/backups`\n\nValidate that a backup destination is in an approved EU region; a non-EU destination fails within 30 seconds with a residency violation and writes nothing (US-001-02)\n\n**Request:** \n- `destination_region` (string, required): Region of the backup storage destination\n- `destination` (string): Identifier of the backup storage target\n**Response:** \n- `valid` (boolean, required): True when the destination is in an approved EU region\n- `destination_region` (string, required): Region that was evaluated\n- `reason` (string): Residency violation detail when rejected\n\n### `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-731179` \u2014 POST `/residency/validations/provisioning`\n\nValidate that a database or compute provisioning request targets an approved EU region (US-001-01)\n\n**Request:** \n- `resource_type` (string, required): Kind of resource being provisioned: database or compute\n- `region` (string, required): Target region of the provisioning request\n**Response:** \n- `valid` (boolean, required): True when the region is an approved EU region\n- `region` (string, required): Region that was evaluated\n- `reason` (string): Explanation when the region is rejected as non-EU\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.invariants | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 6. Invariants\n\n`INV-BE-007` (project spec) also binds this module: a residency check whose region metadata is missing or unreadable fails and blocks deployment. [stories.md US-001-03; design-brief.md AC-001-03-03]\n\n- **INV-BE-160.** An `infra_resource` row has status `pass` only if its type is recognised and its region is non-NULL and in `approved_region` at evaluation time. [requirements.md BR-001; design-brief.md AC-001-04-02]\n- **INV-BE-161.** A refused provisioning or backup validation creates no `infra_resource` row and no `drift_alert` row, and the module never creates a cloud resource or writes backup data. [design-brief.md AC-001-01-02, AC-001-02-02]\n- **INV-BE-162.** Every resource in the latest completed scan appears in the report with a status in `pass`, `fail`, `unverified`. None is omitted. [design-brief.md AC-001-04-01, AC-001-04-02]\n- **INV-BE-163.** The reported percentage is `pass` rows divided by all rows, times 100. It is 100% only if there is at least one row and every row is `pass` (`ASM-BE-459`). [requirements.md NFR-001, NFR-004]\n- **INV-BE-164.** The launch-gate status is `PASSED` if and only if a scan run by that request completed and `INV-BE-163` yields 100%. Otherwise it is `FAILED` (`ASM-BE-460`). [requirements.md BR-003; design-brief.md AC-001-05-01, AC-001-05-02]\n- **INV-BE-165.** For one scan run, at most one `drift_alert` exists per resource. Exactly one exists for each resource whose region was readable and outside `approved_region`, and none otherwise. [design-brief.md AC-001-06-01, AC-001-06-02]\n- **INV-BE-166.** At most one `scan_run` has `state = 'running'` at any time (`ASM-BE-464`).\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.authorization | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 7. Authorization\n\n**Basis.** The role set and the default come from the project auth model. A role has no permission unless an input grants it (`ASM-BE-302`). The existing authentication method is unnamed (`GAP-BE-085`). How pipeline and infrastructure-code callers authenticate, and how these roles are assigned, is unstated (`GAP-BE-472`).\n\n**Legend** (as in the project auth model): `A` allowed, cited. `A*` allowed, derived from a story actor. `-` no grant stated, so refused. `B` blocked on the named item.\n\n| Operation | Platform/DevOps | Release/CI owner | Auditor | Engineering Lead |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-731179` provisioning validation | A* (US-001-01) | - | - | - |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-241176` backup validation | A* (US-001-02) | - | - | - |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-019122` gate check | A* (support, per the project auth model) | A (US-001-03) | - | - |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-913995` compliance report | - | - | A (US-001-04) | - |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094` launch-gate status | - | - | - | `B` (`CON-BE-151`) |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056` on-demand scan | A* (US-001-06) | - | - | - |\n| `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-047792` drift alerts | A (project auth model) | - | - | - |\n\n**Notes.**\n\n- The project auth model had rows for the gate check, report, launch gate and drift alerts only. The rows for provisioning validation, backup validation and on-demand scan are derived from the story actors in US-001-01, US-001-02 and US-001-06. They are not cited grants.\n- The launch-gate cell stays `B`, as in the project spec. Reading the derived status by the Engineering Lead follows from US-001-05, but this module does not release the cell until `Q-BE-152` is answered.\n- Team members and unauthenticated callers have no grant on any operation here, by `ASM-BE-302`.\n- The periodic scan and the launch-gate refresh scan run inside the service under a system identity. They are not an authenticated caller (`GAP-BE-472`).\n- Changes to `approved_region` have no operation and no role (`GAP-BE-477`).\n\n**Ownership predicate.** None. Residency data describes notes_api infrastructure and is not scoped to a user. [backend.domain-model] Derived: the domain model relates INFRA_RESOURCE and DRIFT_ALERT to no User.\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.jobs | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 8. Jobs\n\nNo scheduler is named in any input (`GAP-BE-089`). The jobs below are described by behaviour only.\n\n### 8.1 Periodic residency scan\n\n- **Purpose.** Run the same scan as `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-466056` and raise drift alerts. [stories.md US-001-06; requirements.md BR-004]\n- **Period.** Not stated (`Q-BE-153`). The default is a period no longer than 15 minutes (`ASM-BE-153`, project spec), derived from NFR-003.\n- **Start.** The job is enabled when deployed. No input provides a launch signal, so a non-EU finding before launch also alerts (`ASM-BE-465`, `GAP-BE-478`).\n- **Idempotency and overlap.** At most one scan is `running` (`INV-BE-166`). A tick that finds a scan running does not start another. Alert creation is protected by `UNIQUE (resource_id, scan_run_id)`, so a retried alert step does not duplicate.\n- **Failure.** A failed scan sets `scan_run.state = 'failed'`, is logged, and raises no alert (`Q-BE-452`). The next tick runs normally.\n\n### 8.2 Alert delivery\n\n- **Purpose.** Hand each `PENDING-DELIVERY` alert to the notifier and set `delivered_at` on acknowledgement (`ASM-BE-470`).\n- **Retry.** Pending alerts are retried until acknowledged. The retry interval and limit are unstated (`GAP-BE-479`). The channel and recipients are `GAP-BE-308`.\n- **Idempotency key.** `(resource_id, scan_run_id)`.\n\n### 8.3 Launch-gate refresh scan\n\nTriggered by `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094` with `trigger = 'gate-refresh'`. It follows 8.1's overlap rule. If a scan is already running, the gate status is `FAILED` for that call (`ASM-BE-460`, `GAP-BE-481`).\n\n### 8.4 Sweeps\n\nNone. No input states retention for this module's data (`GAP-BE-480`). No row is deleted by this module.\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 9. Test plan\n\n**Unit** (table-driven, no database).\n\n- `test-residency-evaluate-status-matrix`. Each row of section 3.2: readable non-EU, unrecognised type with readable EU region, unrecognised type with unreadable region, recognised type with unreadable region, and recognised type with EU region.\n- `test-residency-evaluate-precedence`. A non-EU unrecognised resource is `fail` and not `unverified`.\n- `test-residency-percentage`. Zero rows is not 100%; mixed rows; all `pass`.\n- `test-residency-gate-verdict`. Empty component list, empty `approved_region`, null region.\n- Clock-injected tests for the 30-second bound (FR-005) and the 15-minute bound (FR-019).\n\n**Integration** (PostgreSQL 16, per platform.md).\n\n- `test-residency-scan-upsert-and-last-seen`. A second scan updates rows, sets `last_seen_scan_id`, and the report reads only the latest completed scan.\n- `test-residency-scan-single-flight`. The partial unique index refuses a second `running` scan (`INV-BE-166`).\n- `test-residency-alert-unique-per-scan`. A retried alert step creates no second row (`INV-BE-165`).\n- `test-residency-refused-validation-writes-nothing` (`INV-BE-161`).\n- `test-residency-launch-gate-scan-failure`. A failed refresh scan gives `FAILED` (`INV-BE-164`).\n- `test-residency-check-constraints`. The `CHECK` constraints in section 2 reject an out-of-set status and an inconsistent `finished_at`.\n\n**Contract.**\n\n- `test-residency-ops-in-openapi`. All seven operation ids appear in the published OpenAPI document with request, response and error schemas. [requirements.md BR-012, NFR-008]\n- `test-residency-error-statuses-documented`. Every refusal and unavailable case has a documented status. This is red until `GAP-BE-473` is closed.\n- `test-residency-no-restated-schema`. Status values are defined once in the contract.\n\n**Not testable yet.** FR-016 (`CON-BE-151`) and FR-021 (`Q-BE-151`).\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.gaps | status: VERIFIED -->\n## 10. Gaps, contradictions, assumptions and questions\n\n**Contradiction check performed.**\n- Story to design: US-001-01 to US-001-06 against AC-001-01-01 to AC-001-06-02. No contradiction.\n- Story to requirement: `CON-BE-151` (project spec).\n- Requirement to requirement: `CON-BE-451`.\n- Design to design: none found.\n\n### Contradictions\n\n| ID | Class | Statement |\n|---|---|---|\n| `CON-BE-151` | Critical (project spec) | BR-003 enforces the launch gate by Engineering Lead sign-off. AC-001-05-01 authorises release from the report alone. Blocks FR-016. Unblocked by `Q-BE-152`. |\n| `CON-BE-451` | non-critical | AC-001-06-02 says the DevOps team is alerted \"immediately\". NFR-003 says within 15 minutes of drift detection. Consequence of \"immediately\": dispatch at scan completion. Consequence of 15 minutes: delivery may lag. Reading used: dispatch at the end of the detecting scan, which satisfies both (FR-019). Neither is chosen as authoritative. |\n\n### Questions\n\n| ID | Class | Question and default |\n|---|---|---|\n| `Q-BE-151` (project spec) | degradable | Supply the approved EU region list. No default list is invented. FR-021 stays `BLOCKED`. |\n| `Q-BE-152` (project spec) | blocking | Does a passed launch gate authorise release, or does the Engineering Lead sign off? Blocks FR-016. |\n| `Q-BE-153` (project spec) | degradable | Scan period. Default is no longer than 15 minutes (`ASM-BE-153`). |\n| `Q-BE-451` | degradable | Should a post-launch scan finding an unreadable region raise a drift alert? Default: no. The status is `fail` and BR-004 covers non-EU regions only. |\n| `Q-BE-452` | degradable | Should a failed scan alert the DevOps team? Default: no, it is logged only. Risk: drift goes undetected while scans fail. |\n\n### Gaps\n\n| ID | What is missing and what it degrades | Closing artefact |\n|---|---|---|\n| `GAP-BE-472` | No authentication for non-human callers (CI pipeline, infrastructure code, scheduler) and no source for assigning the residency roles. Degrades the authorization section. | A description of the existing authentication and role assignment (with `GAP-BE-085`). |\n| `GAP-BE-473` | No status or error code for: a refused verdict, a residency violation, a scan conflict, or an unavailable report. Degrades FR-002, FR-005, FR-011 and the contract. | A status and error-code decision, recorded in `backend.error-model`. |\n| `GAP-BE-474` | The inventory source and how a region is read are not stated. Degrades FR-017 and the scan. | A description of the inventory and region source. |\n| `GAP-BE-475` | The module returns verdicts, and no input says who builds the pipeline step that acts on them, or what a caller does on timeout or no response. Degrades FR-002, FR-005 and FR-008. | A pipeline and infrastructure-code integration decision. |\n| `GAP-BE-476` | A resource that disappears from the inventory has no stated treatment. Default `ASM-BE-468`: the report shows only the latest scan's resources. | A rule for decommissioned resources. |\n| `GAP-BE-477` | No operation or role exists to change `approved_region`. Degrades FR-021. | An approved-list change process (with `Q-BE-151`). |\n| `GAP-BE-478` | No launch signal exists, so \"post-launch\" cannot be enforced. Default `ASM-BE-465`. | A launch-state definition. |\n| `GAP-BE-479` | Alert resolution, re-alert policy, delivery retry interval and limit are unstated. Degrades section 3.4 and 8.2. | An alert lifecycle rule. |\n| `GAP-BE-480` | Retention of `scan_run`, `infra_resource` and `drift_alert` is unstated. NFR-019 covers other record types only. | A retention rule. |\n| `GAP-BE-481` | `API-RESIDENCYCOMPLIANCE-395094` is a GET that starts a scan. No latency target, request timeout or scan-duration bound is stated for it or for the other operations beyond 30 seconds (FR-005) and 15 minutes (FR-019). | An NFR for latency and a method-safety decision. |\n| `GAP-BE-482` | The project migration plan order 007 omits `approved_region` and `scan_run`, and is `BLOCKED` on `GAP-BE-305`. | An updated project migration plan. |\n| `GAP-BE-483` | The gate check evaluates regions asserted by the caller. No input says whether they are cross-checked against the inventory. | A trust decision for caller-supplied regions. |\n| `GAP-BE-484` | Pagination, filtering and ordering of drift alerts and report rows are unstated. Proposal: newest first for alerts. | A collection-parameter decision. |\n\nReferenced from the project spec and unchanged: `GAP-BE-085`, `GAP-BE-089`, `GAP-BE-304`, `GAP-BE-305`, `GAP-BE-307`, `GAP-BE-308`.\n\n### Assumptions\n\n| ID | Assumption | Reason and confirmation |\n|---|---|---|\n| `ASM-BE-456` | Residency data is stored in the application PostgreSQL. The tables and column types in section 2 are proposals. | No input states them. Confirmed by closing `GAP-BE-305`. |\n| `ASM-BE-457` | Recognised types are `database`, `compute` and `backup-storage`. Any other value is unrecognised. | BR-001 names database, compute and backups. The value names are proposals. Confirmed by the product owner. |\n| `ASM-BE-458` | The evaluation precedence in section 3.2. | No input states one. Confirmed by a rule from the product owner. |\n| `ASM-BE-459` | The percentage counts `fail` and `unverified` rows in the denominator. Zero rows is not 100%. | NFR-001 requires 100% of components EU-verified. Confirmed by the product owner. |\n| `ASM-BE-460` | The report is built from the latest completed scan and states its time. The launch-gate operation runs a fresh scan in the same request. A refused or failed scan gives `FAILED`. | NFR-004 requires an accurate report at each gate check. Confirmed by `GAP-BE-481`'s closing artefact. |\n| `ASM-BE-461` | The validation and gate-check operations are stateless verdicts. Callers must treat a refusal, a timeout or no response as failure. | BR-001 Enforcement places enforcement in infrastructure code. Confirmed by `GAP-BE-475`'s closing artefact. |\n| `ASM-BE-462` | An empty component list or an empty `approved_region` is not compliant. | Fail-closed, per AC-001-03-03. |\n| `ASM-BE-463` | One alert per resource per detecting scan. It is dispatched at the end of that scan, with details: resource id, type, detected region and raise time. | AC-001-06-02 and NFR-003. \"Resource details\" is not defined. Confirmed by `GAP-BE-479`'s closing artefact. |\n| `ASM-BE-464` | Scans are serialised. A second start while one runs is refused. | Prevents duplicate alerts and a contradictory report. Confirmed by `GAP-BE-481`'s closing artefact. |\n| `ASM-BE-465` | The periodic job is enabled when deployed. | No launch signal exists. Confirmed by `GAP-BE-478`'s closing artefact. |\n| `ASM-BE-466` | Module-created rows use application-assigned uuids. All instants are `timestamptz`, UTC. | No input states either. Confirmed by the product owner. |\n| `ASM-BE-467` | The gate check ignores resource type and evaluates region only. | AC-001-03-01 to AC-001-03-03 speak of regions only. |\n| `ASM-BE-468` | The report contains the rows whose `last_seen_scan_id` is the latest completed scan. | US-001-04 requires \"every\" component, and no decommissioning rule exists. |\n| `ASM-BE-469` | The 30-second bound applies to the whole backup validation operation. | AC-001-02-02 does not say how the budget is shared with the pipeline. Confirmed by `GAP-BE-481`'s closing artefact. |\n| `ASM-BE-470` | `delivered_at` is set when the notifier acknowledges, and pending alerts are retried until then. | AC-001-06-02 says \"sent\". Delivery proof and channel are unstated (`GAP-BE-308`). |\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.residency-compliance.gaps -->"}, {"id": "backend/modules/api-docs-SPEC.md", "type": "module_spec", "path": "backend/modules/api-docs-SPEC.md", "content": "<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.frontmatter | status: DERIVED -->\n## Frontmatter\n\n```yaml\nid: api-docs\nsurface: backend\nspec_mode: NOT-APPLICABLE\ndoc_status: DRAFT\nblocked_by: [Q-BE-501]\nscope: [API-APIDOCS-098363, API-APIDOCS-827943]\n```\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.purpose | status: DERIVED -->\n## 1. Purpose\n\n**Responsibility.** The `api-docs` module publishes the contract of notes_api to integrators and measures how complete that publication is. The project module map assigns it these four duties. [backend.module-map]\n\n1. **Publish the OpenAPI document.** The document covers every implemented endpoint: CRUD, tagging, search, archive, and email one-time-code login (send and verify). [stories.md US-004-01; requirements.md BR-012, NFR-008] Operation `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n2. **Compute and expose the endpoint-coverage metric.** The metric is the percentage of implemented endpoints present in the published document, and it names any that are missing. [stories.md US-004-04; requirements.md NFR-008] Operation `API-APIDOCS-827943`.\n3. **Carry the documentation content the stories assign to the document.**\n   - Documented request and response schemas with required fields, types and constraints. [stories.md US-004-02]\n   - Documented 4xx and 5xx responses. [stories.md US-004-03]\n   - Example payloads for search and tagging, including an empty-result example. [stories.md US-004-05; requirements.md NFR-009]\n   - The data-residency note on where active and archived note data is stored and served. [stories.md US-004-06; requirements.md BR-013]\n   - The Scope section listing mobile apps, attachments and real-time collaboration as excluded. [stories.md US-005-01; backend.module-map]\n   - The statement that the `pinned` field, the pinned-first ordering and both login operations are interface changes. [requirements.md NFR-014, NFR-015, NFR-016]\n4. **Guard the document's integrity at build time.** The module owns the spec-validation check and the Scope-section lint. [stories.md US-004-02 (third criterion), US-005-01 (third criterion)]\n\n**Operations owned.** `API-APIDOCS-098363` (GET `/openapi.json`) and `API-APIDOCS-827943` (GET `/openapi/coverage`). Both paths are placeholders pending the path decision. [GAP-BE-303] Their contracts are rendered by DevAgent in `backend.api-docs.api-contract`.\n\n**Not responsible for.**\n\n- **Endpoint schemas, constraints and examples.** The module documents them. It does not define them. Each is defined once by the module that owns the endpoint and is carried into the document by reference. This applies to `notes`, `search` and `auth`. [core rule 6]\n- **The error envelope and the error-code registry.** These belong to `backend.error-model`. The document describes them and does not redefine them. [ASM-BE-300]\n- **Runtime rejection of excluded endpoints.** This belongs to `scope-guardrail`. This module only keeps excluded categories out of the document and shows the Scope section.\n- **Residency scanning, the compliance report and the launch gate.** These belong to `residency-compliance`.\n- **Authentication and the resend and lockout rules.** These belong to `auth`. This module documents their outward behaviour only.\n- **Logging and classifying stakeholder requests.** This is a product-management process. [requirements.md BR-017]\n- **Any user interface that renders the documentation.** No input specifies one.\n- **Choice of OpenAPI generator, validator or CI system.** No input names one. [GAP-BE-089]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 2. Data model\n\n**No tables.** This module owns no persistent entity. The project domain model assigns it none, and the project migration plan has no `api-docs` migration. [backend.domain-model, backend.migration-plan]\n\nThe document is generated at build time from the route registry and the owning modules' contracts. The coverage figure is computed from the document and the route registry on each request. Neither is stored. [ASM-BE-506] The route registry is the comparison source named by the BR-012 enforcement text. [requirements.md BR-012]\n\n| Item | Stored | Note |\n|---|---|---|\n| Published OpenAPI document | build artefact, not a database row | Generated per build. [ASM-BE-506] |\n| Coverage figure | not stored | Computed on request. Whether history is kept is open. [GAP-BE-522] |\n| Data-handling note, Scope section | text inside the document | [ASM-BE-509] |\n\n**Indexes, constraints, columns, foreign keys.** None apply.\n\n**PII.** The module stores none. Example payloads in the document use illustrative values only. Any email, code or token in an example is masked or obviously synthetic. [core section 12]\n\n**Retention and encryption.** No input states either for this module's artefacts. No claim is made. [GAP-BE-304]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 3. State machine\n\nNo entity has a status column. Two derived, per-attempt flows are specified. Each attempt ends in a terminal state and nothing persists between attempts.\n\n### 3.1 Publication of the document, per build\n\nStates: `BUILDING`, `PUBLISHED` (terminal), `REJECTED` (terminal).\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard | Effect |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| none | `BUILDING` | build starts | none | document generated. [ASM-BE-506] |\n| `BUILDING` | `REJECTED` | Scope section absent | none | build fails with a lint or validation error. [US-005-01 third criterion] |\n| `BUILDING` | `REJECTED` | spec-validation finds a mismatch with the code | field type differs from the documented one | CI check fails. [US-004-02 third criterion; GAP-BE-528] |\n| `BUILDING` | `REJECTED` | document does not parse | none | build fails. [US-004-04 third criterion] |\n| `BUILDING` | `PUBLISHED` | all guards pass | Scope section present, document parses, validation passes | document becomes retrievable at or before the new endpoint becomes callable. [US-004-01 second criterion] |\n\nWhether coverage below 100% also moves `BUILDING` to `REJECTED` is open. [GAP-BE-532]\n\n### 3.2 One coverage evaluation\n\nStates: `COMPUTED` (terminal), `PARSE-FAILED` (terminal).\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard | Effect |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| none | `COMPUTED` | `API-APIDOCS-827943` called | document parses | percentage reported, plus missing endpoints if below 100%. [US-004-04 first and second criteria] |\n| none | `PARSE-FAILED` | `API-APIDOCS-827943` called | document malformed | fails with a clear parsing error; no coverage figure is reported. [US-004-04 third criterion] |\n\n**Illegal or unspecified transitions.**\n\n- Calling `API-APIDOCS-098363` before any document has been published: response not stated. [GAP-BE-523]\n- Unsupported Accept header on `API-APIDOCS-098363`: handled by FR-003. [GAP-BE-309; ASM-BE-507]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 4. Requirements\n\nEach requirement carries its traces, verification and API. Test names are proposals.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-001 Serve the OpenAPI document\n\n**Given** notes_api is deployed and a document has been published,\n**When** a client calls `API-APIDOCS-098363`,\n**Then** a valid OpenAPI 3.x JSON or YAML document is returned.\n\n- **Content required.** The document lists all CRUD, tagging, search, archive and email one-time-code login (send and verify) endpoints. The endpoints are those named in the project spec. [backend.module-map]\n- **Success status.** 200 is assumed. [ASM-BE-508]\n- **Self-inclusion.** BR-012 admits no exception, so the document also lists `API-APIDOCS-098363` and `API-APIDOCS-827943`.\n- **Open points.** The 3.x minor version and the served formats are open. [GAP-BE-524] Who may call is open. [Q-BE-501]\n- **Traces to.** US-004-01; BR-012; NFR-008.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-serve-valid-document` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-002 Document appears no later than the endpoint\n\n**Given** a new endpoint is added to notes_api,\n**When** the spec is regenerated and published,\n**Then** the endpoint appears in the document before or at the same moment it becomes callable. [INV-BE-210]\n\n- **Traces to.** US-004-01 (second criterion); BR-012.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-new-endpoint-listed-before-callable` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-003 Unsupported Accept header\n\n**Given** a client requests the document with an unsupported Accept header,\n**When** `API-APIDOCS-098363` is called,\n**Then** the response is the default-format fallback with a clear content type. [ASM-BE-507]\n\n- The 406 alternative is permitted by the story and is not used. [GAP-BE-309]\n- **Traces to.** US-004-01 (third criterion).\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-accept-unsupported-fallback` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-004 Document request and response schemas\n\n**Given** an endpoint accepts or returns a note, tag, search or login payload,\n**When** its schema is inspected in the document,\n**Then** all required fields, types and constraints are documented.\n\n- **Source of the schemas.** They are carried by reference from the owning module's contract. This module defines none. [core rule 6]\n- **Constraint values.** Constraints such as title length and tag format are documented as the owning modules define them. No input supplies them. [GAP-BE-035]\n- **Traces to.** US-004-02 (first criterion); NFR-008.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-schemas-complete` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-005 Optional parameters and defaults\n\n**Given** an endpoint supports optional query parameters, such as the search filters,\n**When** the document is reviewed,\n**Then** each optional parameter is marked optional and its default is documented.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-004-02 (second criterion).\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-optional-params-defaults` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-006 Spec-validation check\n\n**Given** a documented field type is changed in code,\n**When** the document is not updated to match,\n**Then** a spec-validation CI check fails and flags the mismatch. [INV-BE-211]\n\n- **Open points.** What counts as a mismatch, and when the check runs, is open. [GAP-BE-528]\n- **Traces to.** US-004-02 (third criterion).\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-ci-type-mismatch-fails` (planned).\n- **API.** none (build-time check).\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-007 Document the 400 response\n\n**Given** an endpoint can return validation errors,\n**When** the document is reviewed,\n**Then** a 400 response with the error structure and fields is documented.\n\n- The structure is the envelope in `backend.error-model`, referenced and not restated. [ASM-BE-300; GAP-BE-300]\n- **Traces to.** US-004-03 (first criterion); BR-012.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-400-documented` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-008 Document authentication, resend and lock responses\n\n**Given** an endpoint requires authentication or authorisation, or is a login endpoint,\n**When** the document is reviewed,\n**Then**\n- 401 and 403 responses are documented with the expected body shape;\n- the send operation documents the 415 response for more than 3 requests within 10 minutes;\n- the verify operation documents the locked-account response after 5 wrong codes, locked for 15 minutes.\n\n- **Locked-account status.** The status is not stated. [GAP-BE-038]\n- **Wrong, expired and already-used rejections.** Their statuses are not stated. [GAP-BE-053]\n- **Which operations require authentication.** No input names them. The auth-model derivation is used. [backend.auth-model]\n- **Locked-account response on the send operation.** The story can be read as placing it on both login endpoints. BR-032 says requesting a code is not refused during a lock. [CON-BE-501]\n- **Traces to.** US-004-03 (second criterion); BR-027; BR-028; BR-032; NFR-015; NFR-016.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-auth-and-login-errors-documented` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-009 Documented 404 matches behaviour\n\n**Given** an endpoint can return 404,\n**When** the documented 404 response is checked against the actual API,\n**Then** they match. [INV-BE-211]\n\n- The criterion's own preamble does not fit its outcome. [GAP-BE-530]\n- **Traces to.** US-004-03 (third criterion); BR-012.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-404-matches-behaviour` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-010 Coverage at 100%\n\n**Given** all implemented endpoints are documented,\n**When** `API-APIDOCS-827943` is called,\n**Then** it reports 100% coverage.\n\n- **Unit of count.** An endpoint is a method and path pair. [ASM-BE-510]\n- **Success status.** 200 is assumed. [ASM-BE-508]\n- **Traces to.** US-004-04 (first criterion); NFR-008.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-coverage-100` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-827943`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-011 Coverage below 100% names the gaps\n\n**Given** one endpoint is implemented but missing from the document,\n**When** `API-APIDOCS-827943` is called,\n**Then** it reports less than 100% and names the missing endpoint. [INV-BE-212]\n\n- A documented endpoint that is not implemented is not handled by the story. [GAP-BE-531]\n- **Traces to.** US-004-04 (second criterion).\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-coverage-names-missing` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-827943`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-012 Malformed document\n\n**Given** the document is malformed,\n**When** `API-APIDOCS-827943` is called,\n**Then** it fails with a clear parsing error and does not report a coverage figure. [INV-BE-212]\n\n- The failure status and body are not stated. [GAP-BE-523]\n- **Traces to.** US-004-04 (third criterion).\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-coverage-malformed-fails` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-827943`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-013 Examples for search and tagging\n\n**Given** the search and tagging endpoints are documented,\n**When** a developer views the document,\n**Then** each has an example request, including query parameters for search, and an example response body. The search endpoint also has an example empty-result response.\n\n- Example values come from the owning module's contract and are illustrative. They contain no real-looking PII. [core section 12]\n- The tagging paths are not yet supplied, so its examples cannot yet be written. [GAP-BE-303]\n- **Traces to.** US-004-05; NFR-009.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-search-tagging-examples` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-014 Data-handling note\n\n**Given** the document is published,\n**When** an operations engineer reviews it,\n**Then** it states that all note data is stored and served within the EU. [BR-001; BR-013]\n\n- **Archived versus active.** Both are rows of one NOTE entity distinguished by a status column. Storage and serving therefore do not differ, and the note carries no call-out of a difference. [backend.domain-model]\n- **Scope of the claim.** The note claims note data only. It makes no claim about the email address sent for login codes while its region is open. [Q-BE-005; CON-BE-006]\n- **Traces to.** US-004-06; BR-013; BR-001.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-data-handling-note` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-015 Scope section\n\n**Given** the published document,\n**When** a reader views the Scope section,\n**Then** mobile apps, attachments and real-time collaboration are listed as explicitly excluded.\n\n- **Missing section.** The build fails with a lint or validation error. [INV-BE-213]\n- **Change approval.** A change to the section needs review before merge. This is a repository rule outside the running service. The approver is unnamed. [GAP-BE-529]\n- **Traces to.** US-005-01; BR-016.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-scope-section-present` and `test-apidocs-build-fails-without-scope` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-016 Excluded categories absent\n\n**Given** the document is generated,\n**When** its paths are listed,\n**Then** it contains no attachment, real-time or mobile-specific endpoint. [INV-BE-214]\n\n- The `scope-guardrail` module owns the CI contract test that asserts this against the document.\n- **Traces to.** BR-014; BR-015; NFR-010; US-005-05.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-no-excluded-endpoints` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n\n### BE-API-DOCS-FR-017 Interface-change statements\n\n**Given** the `pinned` field, the pinned-first ordering and both login operations,\n**When** the document is generated,\n**Then** each is documented as an interface change for which consumers must adapt.\n\n- The login operations are an additive change; existing login methods are unchanged. [NFR-016]\n- The mechanism for notifying consumers is not stated. [GAP-BE-525]\n- **Traces to.** NFR-014; NFR-015; NFR-016.\n- **Verification.** `test-apidocs-interface-change-documented` (planned).\n- **API.** `API-APIDOCS-098363`.\n\n### Story traceability\n\n| Story | Covered by |\n|---|---|\n| US-004-01 | FR-001, FR-002, FR-003 |\n| US-004-02 | FR-004, FR-005, FR-006 |\n| US-004-03 | FR-007, FR-008, FR-009 |\n| US-004-04 | FR-010, FR-011, FR-012 |\n| US-004-05 | FR-013 |\n| US-004-06 | FR-014 |\n\nAll six stories owned by this module are covered. FR-015 to FR-017 carry the Scope section and interface-change duties that the module map assigns here. The path and supported Accept formats are open (GAP-BE-303, GAP-BE-309); this path is a placeholder pending a path decision. |\n| `API-APIDOCS-827943` | GET | `/openapi/coverage` | `api-docs` | 200 | 401, 403 | Report the endpoint-coverage metric: the percentage of implemented endpoints present in the published OpenAPI document, listing any missing ones. Path is a placeholder (GAP-BE-303). |\n\n### `API-APIDOCS-098363` \u2014 GET `/openapi.json`\n\nRetrieve the published OpenAPI document covering all implemented endpoints, including both login operations and the pinned field, with the data-residency note and the Scope section. The path and supported Accept formats are open (GAP-BE-303, GAP-BE-309); this path is a placeholder pending a path decision.\n\n**Request:** \n- `Accept` (string): Requested representation of the document. Behaviour for an unsupported Accept header (406 or default-format fallback) is undecided (GAP-BE-309).\n**Response:** \n- `openapi` (string, required): OpenAPI document version identifier.\n- `info` (object, required): API metadata, including the data-residency note stating that all note data, active and archived, is stored and served in the EU.\n- `paths` (object, required): All implemented endpoints, including the login operations (POST /auth/otp/send, POST /auth/otp/verify) and the pinned field on notes, each with documented 401/403 responses where authentication is required and example payloads for search and tagging endpoints.\n- `components` (object): Reusable schemas, including the error envelope.\n- `scope` (object, required): Scope section listing excluded capabilities: attachments, real-time channels and mobile-specific endpoints, with the out-of-scope error behaviour.\n\n### `API-APIDOCS-827943` \u2014 GET `/openapi/coverage`\n\nReport the endpoint-coverage metric: the percentage of implemented endpoints present in the published OpenAPI document, listing any missing ones. Path is a placeholder (GAP-BE-303).\n\n**Request:** none\n**Response:** \n- `coverage_percent` (number, required): Percentage of implemented endpoints present in the published OpenAPI spec (target 100%, NFR-008).\n- `implemented_count` (integer, required): Number of implemented endpoints.\n- `documented_count` (integer, required): Number of implemented endpoints present in the published spec.\n- `missing_endpoints` (array, required): Implemented endpoints absent from the published spec; empty at 100% coverage.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.invariants | status: DERIVED -->\n## 6. Invariants\n\n- **INV-BE-210.** No endpoint is callable unless it appears in the published document, and it appears no later than it becomes callable. [US-004-01 second criterion; BR-012]\n- **INV-BE-211.** No published document disagrees with the implemented field types or documented error responses (including 404) of any documented endpoint. [US-004-02 third criterion; US-004-03 third criterion]\n- **INV-BE-212.** A coverage figure below 100% always names every missing endpoint, and no figure is produced from a malformed document. [US-004-04 second and third criteria]\n- **INV-BE-213.** Every published document contains the Scope section and the data-handling note. A build lacking the Scope section is not published. [US-005-01 third criterion; BR-013]\n- **INV-BE-214.** The published document lists no attachment, real-time or mobile-specific endpoint. [BR-014, BR-015, BR-016]\n- **INV-BE-215.** Neither `API-APIDOCS-098363` nor `API-APIDOCS-827943` changes any note, tag, user, code, send-attempt or lock record. Both are reads. [derived from method GET; backend.module-map]\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.authorization | status: BLOCKED -->\n## 7. Authorization\n\n**Blocked.** No input states who may call `API-APIDOCS-098363` or `API-APIDOCS-827943`.\n\n- The stories name three groups of intended users.\n  - Integration developers: US-004-01, US-004-03, US-004-05.\n  - Backend developers: US-004-04.\n  - Operations engineers: US-004-06.\n- None of those stories gives a credential precondition, and the project auth model lists the document operation as not stated. [backend.auth-model; GAP-BE-309]\n- Under the default rule, no role has permission without a grant. [ASM-BE-302]\n- Whether the operations are public, available to any authenticated caller, or restricted to roles changes an authorisation rule. It is therefore a blocking question. [Q-BE-501]\n\n**What would unblock it.** An answer to `Q-BE-501`. Both operations are system-wide resources and are not user-scoped, so no ownership predicate is expected.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.api-docs.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.api-docs.jobs | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 8. Jobs\n\nNo scheduled sweep is stated for this module in any input.\n\n| Job | Trigger | Purpose | Source |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Document generation and validation | each build | generate the document, run the Scope-section lint and the spec-validation check, and block publication on failure | [US-005-01 third criterion; US-004-02 third criterion] |\n| Coverage check | CI, and on demand through `API-APIDOCS-827943` | compare the document with the route registry | [BR-012 enforcement text; US-004-04] |\n\n- **Timing.** When the coverage check runs in CI is not stated. [GAP-BE-528]\n- **Retries.** None are stated, and none are assumed.\n- **Idempotency keys.** None are needed. Both operations are GET reads, and a build is regenerated from source. [INV-BE-215]\n- **History.** Whether coverage results are stored over time is open.
