[project spec backend.module-map]\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.frontmatter -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.purpose | status: DERIVED -->\n## 1. Purpose\n\n**Responsibility.** The module intercepts every inbound request before routing and rejects calls to excluded capabilities. [project spec backend.module-map]\n\n1. Reject attachment and file-upload calls: attachment paths and multipart payloads sent to note endpoints. [stories.md US-005-02; requirements.md BR-014]\n2. Reject real-time channels: WebSocket upgrade and SSE connections. [stories.md US-005-03; requirements.md BR-015]\n3. Reject mobile-specific endpoint calls. [requirements.md BR-016; stories.md US-005-04]\n4. Return a structured, documented out-of-scope error: a code plus a human-readable reason that references the scope exclusion. [stories.md US-005-04; requirements.md BR-016, NFR-011]\n5. Back the contract test suite that runs on every CI build. It passes only when zero endpoints match an excluded category, and it blocks the merge of a pull request that adds one. [stories.md US-005-05; requirements.md NFR-010]\n\n**Position in the request path.** The guardrail runs first, then `auth`, then `notes`, `search` or `api-docs`. [project spec backend.module-map]\n\n**Stories.** `US-005-02`, `US-005-03`, `US-005-04` and `US-005-05` are covered here, as the project coverage table assigns them.\n\n**Not responsible for.**\n\n- The Scope section of the published documentation, `US-005-01`. That belongs to `api-docs`. [project spec backend.coverage]\n- Logging and classifying stakeholder feature requests, `US-005-06`. That is a product-management process, not service behaviour. [requirements.md BR-017; project spec backend.coverage; GAP-BE-001]\n- Authentication, authorisation and the credential. Those belong to `auth`. [project spec backend.auth-model]\n- Concurrent PATCH behaviour on one note (`US-005-03` AC2). That belongs to `notes`; see `GAP-BE-575`.\n- The global error envelope and the registry of error codes. Those belong to `backend.error-model`. This module references them and does not redefine them.\n- The OpenAPI document itself (`api-docs`). The module only supplies the exclusion rules that the contract test applies to it.\n- Any persisted data. See the data model.\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.purpose -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.data-model | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 2. Data model\n\n**Tables.** The module owns none. The project domain model assigns no entity to it, and the project migration plan has no migration for it. [project spec backend.domain-model, backend.migration-plan] A rejected request persists nothing; see `INV-BE-262`.\n\n**Exclusion registry (configuration, not a table).** The module needs a list of excluded categories and their match rules. No input defines its shape or says where it is stored. The shape below is a proposal, `ASM-BE-556`.\n\n| Field | Type | Null | Meaning |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `category` | enum: `attachment`, `real-time`, `mobile` | NOT NULL | The three exclusion categories. [stories.md US-005-01, US-005-05; requirements.md BR-016] |\n| `match_kind` | enum: `path-segment`, `protocol-upgrade`, `event-stream`, `multipart-body` | NOT NULL | How a request is matched (`ASM-BE-557`). |\n| `match_value` | text | NULL for protocol kinds | The segment, for example `attachments` or `ws`. |\n| `response_code` | text | NOT NULL | The error code returned (see `GAP-BE-573`). |\n\n**Seed values taken from the inputs.**\n\n| Category | Value | Source |\n|---|---|---|\n| `attachment` | path segment `attachments`, as in `/notes/{id}/attachments` and `/attachments` | [stories.md US-005-02, US-005-05] |\n| `real-time` | path segment `ws`, as in `/ws` | [stories.md US-005-05] |\n| `real-time` | WebSocket upgrade; SSE connection | [stories.md US-005-03] |\n| `attachment` | multipart/form-data payload to a note create or update endpoint | [stories.md US-005-02 AC2] |\n| `mobile` | none supplied | `GAP-BE-572` |\n\n**Indexes and constraints.** None. The registry is a small static list read on every request, and no input gives its size.\n\n**PII.** None. The module reads request metadata only. Treatment of request paths in logs is not stated: `GAP-BE-574`.\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.data-model -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.state-machine | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 3. State machine\n\nThe module keeps no durable state. Two short-lived machines are specified.\n\n### 3.1 Inbound request\n\nStates: `RECEIVED`, `OUT-OF-SCOPE`, `IN-SCOPE`, `REJECTED` (terminal), `FORWARDED` (terminal for this module).\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard | Effect |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| none | `RECEIVED` | request arrives | none | classification starts, before authentication |\n| `RECEIVED` | `OUT-OF-SCOPE` | a registry rule matches | order in `ASM-BE-558` | none yet |\n| `RECEIVED` | `IN-SCOPE` | no rule matches | none | none yet |\n| `OUT-OF-SCOPE` | `REJECTED` | classification done | none | structured error returned; protocol never upgraded; nothing persisted (`INV-BE-260`, `INV-BE-262`, `INV-BE-263`, `INV-BE-264`) |\n| `IN-SCOPE` | `FORWARDED` | classification done | none | handed to `auth`, then the owning module |\n\n**Terminal states.** `REJECTED` and `FORWARDED`. A rejected request never reaches a handler (`INV-BE-260`).\n\n**Unmatched mobile-specific calls.** With no mobile pattern defined (`GAP-BE-572`), such a call is classified `IN-SCOPE` and falls through to ordinary routing. Whatever response routing gives an unknown path is then outside this module's control.\n\n### 3.2 Contract-test run (CI)\n\nStates: `STARTED`, `PASSED` (terminal), `FAILED` (terminal).\n\n| From | To | Trigger | Guard | Effect |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| none | `STARTED` | CI build runs | none | the suite reads the OpenAPI document and the route registry [stories.md US-005-02, US-005-05] |\n| `STARTED` | `PASSED` | check ends | zero endpoints match an excluded category | merge allowed |\n| `STARTED` | `FAILED` | check ends | one or more endpoints match, for example `/attachments` or `/ws` | pull request merge blocked [stories.md US-005-05 AC2] |\n\nAn unreadable OpenAPI document is treated as `FAILED`. No input states this. It is derived from the fail-closed stance of `AC-001-03-03` and recorded as `ASM-BE-559`.\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.state-machine -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.requirements | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 4. Requirements\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-001 Reject attachment paths\n\n**Given** a request to any attachment path, for example POST `/notes/{id}/attachments`,\n**When** the guardrail classifies it,\n**Then** the request is rejected with a structured out-of-scope error, no handler runs and no file content is persisted.\n\n- **Status.** The response form is open: a bare 404 (US-005-02) versus a message that references the scope exclusion (US-005-04, BR-016). See `CON-BE-007`. This module does not choose.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-02 AC1; BR-014; BR-016.\n- **Verification.** `test-guardrail-attachment-path-rejected` (planned).\n- **API.** none.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-002 Reject multipart payloads to note endpoints\n\n**Given** a multipart/form-data request carrying a file payload to a note create or update endpoint (`ASM-BE-560`),\n**When** the guardrail classifies it,\n**Then** the request returns 415 and persists no file content.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-005-02 AC2; US-005-04 AC2; BR-014.\n- **Verification.** `test-guardrail-multipart-415-no-persist` (planned).\n- **Note.** The error code is `GAP-BE-573`. The shared use of 415 is distinguished by code in `backend.error-model`.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-003 Reject real-time connections\n\n**Given** a client attempts a WebSocket upgrade or an SSE connection to any notes_api endpoint,\n**When** the guardrail classifies it,\n**Then** the connection is rejected, no protocol upgrade occurs and a standard error status is returned.\n\n- **Status.** The exact status is not stated (`GAP-BE-309`, project spec). Detection is `ASM-BE-557`.\n- **Traces to.** US-005-03 AC1; BR-015; NFR-010.\n- **Verification.** `test-guardrail-websocket-sse-rejected` (planned).\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-004 Structured out-of-scope response\n\n**Given** any request classified out of scope,\n**When** the guardrail rejects it,\n**Then** the response body carries a structured error code and a human-readable reason that references the scope exclusion, and not a bare 404 or a silent failure.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-005-04 AC1, AC2; BR-016; NFR-011 (100% of out-of-scope calls return a documented 4xx with an explanatory message).\n- **Verification.** `test-guardrail-response-shape` (planned).\n- **Note.** The envelope is `backend.error-model` (`ASM-BE-300`). The message text is `GAP-BE-576`.\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-005 Reject mobile-specific calls\n\n**Given** a call to a mobile-specific endpoint,\n**When** the guardrail classifies it,\n**Then** it is rejected with the structured response of FR-004.\n\n- **Status.** `BLOCKED`. No input defines what a mobile-specific endpoint or path looks like (`GAP-BE-572`).\n- **Traces to.** BR-016; US-005-04; US-005-05.\n- **Verification.** `test-guardrail-mobile-rejected` (planned, once defined).\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-006 Guardrail precedes authentication\n\n**Given** an out-of-scope call with no credential,\n**When** it arrives,\n**Then** it receives the documented out-of-scope response and not an authentication failure.\n\n- **Status.** `DERIVED` from the module map's request order. Whether out-of-scope calls are authenticated is not stated in any input. [project spec backend.module-map]\n- **Traces to.** BR-016; NFR-011.\n- **Verification.** `test-guardrail-before-auth` (planned).\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-007 Contract test passes only with zero excluded endpoints\n\n**Given** the CI pipeline runs,\n**When** the scope-guardrail test suite executes against the OpenAPI spec,\n**Then** it passes only when zero endpoints match an excluded category (mobile, attachments, real-time). It runs on every CI build.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-005-05 AC1; BR-014; BR-015; NFR-010.\n- **Verification.** `test-contract-no-excluded-endpoints` (planned).\n\n### BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-008 Pull request adding an excluded endpoint is blocked\n\n**Given** a developer adds a new endpoint matching an excluded pattern, for example `/attachments` or `/ws`,\n**When** CI runs on the pull request,\n**Then** the guardrail test fails and the merge is blocked.\n\n- **Traces to.** US-005-05 AC2; NFR-010.\n- **Verification.** `test-contract-pr-with-excluded-route-fails` (planned).\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.requirements -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.api-contract | status: VERIFIED -->\n## Api contract\n\n_Rendered by DevAgent from the operation records._\n\nNo HTTP operation is defined.\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.api-contract -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.invariants | status: DERIVED -->\n## 6. Invariants\n\n- **INV-BE-260.** A request classified out of scope never reaches a module handler or any persistence layer. [stories.md US-005-02; requirements.md BR-014, BR-015]\n- **INV-BE-261.** No endpoint in the published OpenAPI document matches an excluded category. [requirements.md NFR-010; stories.md US-005-05]\n- **INV-BE-262.** A rejected multipart or attachment request leaves no stored file content. [stories.md US-005-02 AC2]\n- **INV-BE-263.** Every out-of-scope response carries a structured error code and an explanatory message. [requirements.md BR-016, NFR-011; stories.md US-005-04]\n- **INV-BE-264.** A rejected WebSocket or SSE request never completes a protocol upgrade or opens a stream. [stories.md US-005-03 AC1]\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.invariants -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.authorization | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 7. Authorization\n\nThe module owns no operation, so no role invokes one. The only permission statement that applies is the project matrix row for out-of-scope paths. [project spec backend.auth-model]\n\n| Resource and operation | Unauth. caller | Team member | Platform/DevOps | Release/CI owner | Auditor | Eng. Lead |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| Any out-of-scope path (attachments, WebSocket or SSE, mobile) | D | D | D | D | D | D |\n\n`D` means refused, cited. [requirements.md BR-014, BR-015, BR-016]\n\n**Ownership predicate.** None. The module touches no user-scoped resource.\n\n**Classification and credentials.** The guardrail decides before any credential is checked (`BE-SCOPE-GUARDRAIL-FR-006`), so refusal does not depend on the caller's identity.\n\n**Contract test.** Who may run, or override a failure of, the CI check is not stated in any input. Merge blocking is enforced by CI, which is outside this service. [stories.md US-005-05]\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.authorization -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.jobs | status: DERIVED -->\n## 8. Jobs\n\n- **Scheduled work.** None. No input states a sweep, schedule or retry for this module.\n- **CI contract test.** It runs on every CI build and on pull requests. It is a pipeline trigger, not a scheduled job. [requirements.md NFR-010; stories.md US-005-05]\n- **Retries.** None. Classification is stateless and runs inline.\n- **Idempotency keys.** None. Classification has no side effects other than the rejection response, so repeated identical requests give identical results.\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.jobs -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.test-plan | status: DRAFT-UNVERIFIED -->\n## 9. Test plan\n\n**Unit.**\n\n- `test-guardrail-classify-attachment-segment`: matches `attachments` as a path segment, and not as a substring of a note id.\n- `test-guardrail-classify-ws-segment`: matches `/ws`.\n- `test-guardrail-match-order` (`ASM-BE-558`): path and protocol checks run before the content-type check.\n- `test-guardrail-registry-unknown-category`: a registry entry with an unknown category fails at load.\n\n**Integration.**\n\n- `test-guardrail-attachment-path-rejected` (FR-001).\n- `test-guardrail-multipart-415-no-persist` (FR-002): asserts that no file content is stored (`INV-BE-262`).\n- `test-guardrail-websocket-sse-rejected` (FR-003): asserts no upgrade (`INV-BE-264`).\n- `test-guardrail-response-shape` (FR-004): asserts a code and a message (`INV-BE-263`).\n- `test-guardrail-before-auth` (FR-006): sends no credential.\n- `test-guardrail-in-scope-passthrough`: a note request reaches its handler unchanged.\n\n**Contract.**\n\n- `test-contract-no-excluded-endpoints` (FR-007, `INV-BE-261`).\n- `test-contract-pr-with-excluded-route-fails` (FR-008).\n- `test-contract-unreadable-spec-fails` (`ASM-BE-559`).\n- Error-model check: the guardrail's response codes appear in the `backend.error-model` registry, and the 415 for a multipart request is distinguishable from the 415 for the resend limit by code.\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.test-plan -->\n<!-- SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.gaps | status: DERIVED -->\n## 10. Gaps\n\n**Referenced from the project spec, not redefined.** `CON-BE-007` (404 versus scope message), `GAP-BE-001` (US-005-06 has no backend module), `GAP-BE-309` (WebSocket and SSE rejection status), `ASM-BE-300` (error envelope), `ASM-BE-301` (error code names), `CON-BE-019`. `CON-BE-007` is non-critical in the project spec.\n\n**Gaps (new).**\n\n| ID | Gap | Degrades | Closing artefact |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `GAP-BE-572` | No input defines a mobile-specific endpoint or path pattern, so FR-005 and the mobile contract test cannot be written. | FR-005, FR-007 | a list of mobile path patterns or headers |\n| `GAP-BE-573` | Which error code a multipart rejection returns is unresolved. US-005-04 AC2 gives `OUT_OF_SCOPE` as an example. The project registry proposes `UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE` at 415. Default applied in this module: use the project registry's code, as `Q-BE-551` (degradable) records. | FR-002 | an error-code decision |\n| `GAP-BE-574` | Whether rejected requests are logged or metered, and how request paths are treated in logs, is not stated. | observability | an observability decision |\n| `GAP-BE-575` | US-005-03 AC2 asks for a \"standard last-write/conflict response\" on concurrent PATCH. It is not stated whether the response is last-write or conflict, nor the conflict status. The behaviour belongs to `notes`. That module should state it; this module does not specify it. | notes PATCH | a concurrency decision |\n| `GAP-BE-576` | The wording of the out-of-scope message is not supplied. Only that it must reference the scope exclusion. | FR-004 | message text |\n\n**Question.**\n\n| ID | Class | Question | Default applied |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `Q-BE-551` | degradable | Which error code does a multipart rejection use: `OUT_OF_SCOPE` or `UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE`? | `UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE`, as in the project registry (`GAP-BE-573`) |\n\n**Assumptions.**\n\n| ID | Assumption | Reasoning | Confirmed by |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `ASM-BE-556` | The exclusion registry is static configuration and has no database tables. | Nothing in the inputs requires persisted guardrail data. | an architecture decision |\n| `ASM-BE-557` | A WebSocket upgrade is detected from the HTTP `Upgrade` header, and an SSE connection from an `Accept` of `text/event-stream`. | US-005-03 names the mechanisms but no input states how they are recognised. | an implementation decision |\n| `ASM-BE-558` | Classification order is path and protocol checks first, then content-type checks. | A single request can match more than one rule, for example an attachment path carrying multipart, and the response must be predictable. | a decision from the product owner |\n| `ASM-BE-559` | An unreadable OpenAPI document fails the contract test. | It follows the fail-closed rule of US-001-03 AC3 and US-004-04 AC3 (a malformed spec fails rather than reporting false coverage). | confirmation |\n| `ASM-BE-560` | The multipart rule applies to POST `/notes` and PATCH `/notes/{id}`, the note create and update operations in the project api-surface. | US-005-02 says \"a note create/update endpoint\" and no input lists them by path. | the final operation list from `notes` |\n\n<!-- /SECTION: backend.scope-guardrail.gaps -->"}, {"id": "backend/contracts/openapi.yaml", "type": "openapi", "path": "backend/contracts/openapi.yaml", "content": "openapi: 3.1.0\ninfo:\n  title: Backend API\n  version: 1.0.0\n  description: Operations the backend module specifications define, rendered from\n    their structured operation records.
